193	    if (this.age === 300) {
194	      const hasDragon = (game.enemies() as Enemy[])
195	        .some((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 454);
196	      const id = hasDragon ? 521 : 454;
197	      const head = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, this.cx, this.cy);
198	      if (head) {
199	        addEnemy(game, head);
200	        if (id === 454) {
201	          // 链段（NewNPC 段链展开）：455-458 + 尾 459，realLife 共享头 10000 血
202	          const segs = Enemy.spawnWormChain(head, 5, [455, 456, 457, 458, 459], true);
203	          for (const sg of segs) addEnemy(game, sg);
204	        }
205	      }
206	    }
207	    if (this.age >= 320) this.dead = true;              // :31294-31298
208	  }
209	
210	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
211	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
212	    if (!ctx) return;
213	    const img = projSprite(490);
214	    if (!img || !(img.width > 0) || img.width === 0) return;
215	    const s = this.scale;
216	    if (s <= 0.01) return;
217	    const iw = img.width, ih = img.height;
218	    ctx.save();
219	    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
220	    ctx.rotate(this.rot);
221	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
222	    ctx.drawImage(img, -iw * s / 2, -ih * s / 2, iw * s, ih * s);
223	    ctx.restore();
224	  }
225	}
226	
227	/** 克隆体生成出口(:65646-65650 NewNPC 440:X,Y 是底部中心坐标且 Y 再 +真身 height/2;
228	 *  ai[3]=真身 whoAmI / localAI[1]=仪式代数)。本仓:la3=真身引用、la1=代数、跳过出生显影 */
229	function spawnCultistClone(game: GameHooks, parent: Enemy, x: number, y: number, gen: number): Enemy | null {
230	  const c = spawnPart(game, parent, 440);
231	  if (!c) return null;
232	  const la = laOf(c);
233	  la.la3 = parent;
234	  la.la1 = gen;
235	  c.x = x - c.w / 2;
236	  c.y = y + parent.h / 2 - c.h;
237	  c.bInit = true; // 原版克隆体会落入 1t 的 -1 显影态(:65047),无实际效果,直接跳过
238	  return c;
239	}
240	
241	/** 克隆体补射 468(:65375-65397 冰雾 / :65462-65499 火球第三拍 / :65531-65562 闪电 /
242	 *  :65796-65822 光第三拍):每只克隆体朝玩家(预判 20t)发 count 发,18 伤,
243	 *  (6+rand4) 速 ±spread 散布,出口 = 克隆体中心 + (facing*30, 12) */
244	function cloneSupplement(game: GameHooks, clones: Enemy[], player: Player, count: number, spread: number): void {
245	  for (const c of clones) {
246	    const dir = Math.sign(player.cx - c.cx);
247	    if (dir !== 0) c.facing = dir;
248	    const aim = aimLead(c, player, c.facing);
249	    for (let k = 0; k < count; k++) {
250	      const spd = 6 + Math.random() * 4;
251	      const [dvx, dvy] = rot(aim.x * spd, aim.y * spd, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * spread);
252	      shoot(game, c.cx + c.facing * 30, c.cy + 12, dvx, dvy, 18, 468);
253	    }
254	  }
255	}
256	
257	/** 仪式圈心(原版 Projectile 490 的位置 = 仪式开始时真身中心,:65652)。
258	 *  真身存 la2;克隆体读真身的 la2 */
259	function ritualCenterOf(isReal: boolean, la: { la2: unknown }, parent: Enemy | null): { x: number; y: number } | null {
260	  const raw = (isReal ? la.la2 : (parent ? laOf(parent).la2 : null)) as { x: number; y: number } | null;
261	  return raw ?? null;
262	}
263	
264	export function lunaticCultistAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): void {
265	  const la = laOf(e);
266	  // getGoodWorld 弹幕节奏抬档(:64932-64958):冰 num120−30 / 火 18×3→10×5 /
267	  // 闪电 num80−20(expert 基线未实装,g 照原文在经典值上调整)
268	  const ftw = !!game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld;
269	  const isReal = e.vanillaId !== 440; // flag2 = type==439(:64971);440 = 克隆体
270	  let parent: Enemy | null = null;
271	  // ---- 克隆体镜像段(:64998-65015):真身失效 → 消散;ai0/ai1 逐 tick 镜像;
272	  //      仪式态中被击中 → 自身消散 + 真身进 6 态硬直 ----
273	  if (!isReal) {
274	    parent = (la.la3 as unknown as Enemy) ?? null;
275	    if (!parent || parent.dead || parent.vanillaId !== 439) { // :64975-64982
276	      e.dead = true;
277	      return;
278	    }
279	    e.ai0 = parent.ai0;
280	    e.ai1 = parent.ai1;
281	    if (e.ai0 === 5) {
282	      if (e.justHit) { // :65003-65014
283	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8060D0', 14, 2.4);
284	        e.dead = true;
285	        parent.ai0 = 6;
286	        parent.ai1 = 0;
287	        return;
288	      }
289	    } else {
290	      e.iframes = 2; // flag3/flag4 → dontTakeDamage(chaseable=false,:65015)
291	    }
292	  } else if (!e.bInit) {
293	    // :65047-65057 localAI[0]==0:音效 + alpha=255 + ai[0]=-1(出生显形)
294	    e.bInit = true;
295	    e.ai0 = -1; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
296	    la.la0 = 1;
297	    e.spawnAlpha = 254;
298	    game.playSfxWav?.('Zombie_89', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :65093 PlaySound(29,…,89)——case29 style89 ×0.7 自动档(曾借 roar 0.6)
299	  }
300	  const b = baseOf(e);
301	  const phase2 = e.hp <= e.maxHp / 2;                       // flag :64928
302	  e.def.defense = Math.floor(phase2 ? b.def * 0.65 : b.def); // :64982-64985 半血防御 ×0.65
303	
304	  // :65060-65088 玩家死/超 5600px → 消散退场(连带清全部克隆体,:65078-65087)
305	  if (!player || player.dead || Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > 5600) {
306	    game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#6040A0', 14, 2.4);
307	    if (isReal) {
308	      for (const c of cultistClones(game, e)) {
309	        game.spawnParticles(c.cx, c.cy, '#6040A0', 10, 2);
310	        c.dead = true;
311	      }
312	    }
313	    e.dead = true;
314	    return;
315	  }
316	
317	  // ---- 仪式窗口(120-420t)真身被击中(:65016-65060):推进选招 + 清同代克隆体(≤10) ----
318	  if (isReal && e.ai0 === 5 && e.ai1 >= 120 && e.ai1 < 420 && e.justHit) {
319	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 += 1; // :65021-65025
320	    e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
321	    let budget = 10; // num9 经典 10(expert 3,:65041-65044);两分支同为清场,差异仅联网消息
322	    for (const c of cultistClones(game, e)) {
323	      if (budget <= 0) break;
324	      budget--;
325	      game.spawnParticles(c.cx, c.cy, '#8060D0', 12, 2.4);
326	      c.dead = true;
327	    }
328	  }
329	
330	  // 朝向(:65117-65120 sign(player.X - center.X);仪式态内被 :65676 系圈心朝向覆盖)
331	  const face = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx);
332	  if (face !== 0) e.facing = face;
333	
334	  // ---- ai0 == -1 出生显形(:65059-65109):420t,前段不可选中;300t 起上浮 ----
335	  if (e.ai0 === -1) {
336	    e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, 254 - e.ai1 * 5); // :65066 alpha -= 5/tick
337	    e.iframes = 2; // flag3/flag4 → dontTakeDamage
338	    e.ai1 += 1;
339	    if (e.ai1 > 360) { e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95; }      // :65109-65111 收势阻尼
340	    else if (e.ai1 > 300) { e.vx = 0; e.vy = -1; }        // :65113-65115 velocity=-UnitY
341	    flyIntegrate(e);
342	    if (e.ai1 >= 420) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.spawnAlpha = 0; }
343	    return;
344	  }
345	
346	  // ---- ai0 == 0 选招(:65110-65336):40t 后按 ai3 拍号取招(flag2 门控,克隆体不选) ----
347	  if (e.ai0 === 0) {
348	    e.ai1 += 1;
349	    if (isReal && e.ai1 >= 40) {
350	      e.ai1 = 0;
351	      const pick = cultistPick(e, phase2);
352	      if (pick === 0) {
353	        // 飞掠占位(:65277-65336):真身 + 全部克隆体一起入环——环位角
354	        // num16 = ((序号+flag5+1)/2)·2π·0.4/总数,奇数序取负(flag5=总数偶数),
355	        // 半径椭圆 (300,200),自转轴 (0,-1);num14 = ceil(距离/50) 至少 1,
356	        // velocity = 位移/num14,ai1 = num14*2(隔拍移动;克隆体 ai1 会被镜像覆写为真身值)
357	        const group = [e, ...cultistClones(game, e)];
358	        const flag5 = group.length % 2 === 0 ? 1 : 0; // :65287
359	        let idx = 0;
360	        for (const m of group) {
361	          let num16 = Math.floor((idx + flag5 + 1) / 2) * Math.PI * 2 * 0.4 / group.length; // :65291-65296
362	          if (idx % 2 === 1) num16 = -num16;
363	          if (group.length === 1) num16 = 0;
364	          const [ux, uy] = rot(0, -1, num16);
365	          const destX = player.cx + ux * 300;
366	          const destY = player.cy + uy * 200;
367	          const num14 = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.hypot(destX - m.cx, destY - m.cy) / 50)); // :65279-65283
368	          m.vx = (destX - m.cx) / num14;
369	          m.vy = (destY - m.cy) / num14;
370	          m.ai0 = 1;
371	          m.ai1 = num14 * 2;
372	          // :65318-65321 whoAmI >= 自身 → 选招当帧回退(净位移 0);克隆体按 id 序决定
373	          if (m === e || e.id >= m.id) { m.x -= m.vx; m.y -= m.vy; }
374	          idx++;
375	        }
376	      } else if (pick === 1) { e.ai0 = 3; } // 火球
377	      else if (pick === 2) { e.ai0 = 2; }   // 冰雾
378	      else if (pick === 3) { e.ai0 = 4; }   // 闪电
379	      else if (pick === 4) { e.ai0 = 5; }   // 仪式瞬移
380	      else if (pick === 5) { e.ai0 = 7; }   // 远古之光
381	      // pick === 6(远古末日)仅 expert 随机替换会命中(:65270-65275),经典不触发
382	    }
383	    flyIntegrate(e);
384	    return;
385	  }
386	
387	  // ---- ai0 == 1 飞掠(:65338-65361):奇数拍回退位移 → 实际隔拍移动;期间无敌(flag3) ----
388	  if (e.ai0 === 1) {
389	    e.iframes = 2;
390	    const undo = Math.floor(e.ai1) % 2 !== 0 && e.ai1 !== 1; // :65341-65344
391	    if (!undo) flyIntegrate(e);
392	    e.ai1 -= 1;
393	    if (e.ai1 <= 0) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; if (isReal) e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
394	    return;
395	  }
396	
397	  // ---- ai0 == 2 冰雾 464(:65362-65429):4t 时克隆体各补 1 发 468 + 真身 1 发 464(35 伤);
398	  //      持续 4+num(num=120,FTW −30 :64932-64936) ----
399	  if (e.ai0 === 2) {
400	    const numIce = ftw ? 90 : 120;
401	    if (isReal && e.ai1 >= 4 && (e.ai1 - 4) % numIce === 0) {
402	      cloneSupplement(game, cultistClones(game, e), player, 1, 0.5235987901687622); // :65375-65397
403	      const aim = aimLead(e, player, e.facing);
404	      shoot(game, e.cx + e.facing * 30, e.cy + 12, aim.x * 4, aim.y * 4, 35, 464); // :65411-65421
405	      // 原版冰雾发射零吼(:65040-65900 全段仅 65093/65122 两声)——曾借 roar 0.35,2026-08-18 对账删
406	    }
407	    e.ai1 += 1;
408	    if (e.ai1 >= 4 + numIce) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; if (isReal) e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
409	    return;
410	  }
411	
412	  // ---- ai0 == 3 火球 467(:65430-65530):每 num2t 一发×num3,30 伤,(6+rand4) 速
413	  //      ±0.52rad 散布(18×3,FTW 10×5 :64941-64949);第三拍克隆体各补 1 发
414	  //      468(:65462-65499);持续 4+num2*num3 ----
415	  if (e.ai0 === 3) {
416	    const numFire = ftw ? 10 : 18;
417	    const fireCount = ftw ? 5 : 3;
418	    if (isReal && e.ai1 >= 4 && (e.ai1 - 4) % numFire === 0) {
419	      // 原版硬编码第 3 拍(:65442 /num2==2)——FTW 5 拍下仍在第 3 拍补射,1:1 保留
420	      if ((e.ai1 - 4) / numFire === 2) {
421	        cloneSupplement(game, cultistClones(game, e), player, 1, 0.5235987901687622); // :65448-65462
422	      }
423	      const aim = aimLead(e, player, e.facing);
424	      const spd = 6 + Math.random() * 4;
425	      const [dvx, dvy] = rot(aim.x * spd, aim.y * spd, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * 0.5235987901687622);
426	      shoot(game, e.cx + e.facing * 30, e.cy + 12, dvx, dvy, 30, 467); // :65505-65517
427	    }
428	    e.ai1 += 1;
429	    if (e.ai1 >= 4 + numFire * fireCount) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; if (isReal) e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
430	    return;
431	  }
432	
433	  // ---- ai0 == 4 闪电 465(:65531-65622):ai1==20 时克隆体各补 1 发 468(:65531-65562),
434	  //      之后每逢 (ai1-20)%num4==0 在头顶 100px 撒 465(45 伤)(num4=80,FTW −20
435	  //      :64952-64958);持续 20+num4 ----
436	  if (e.ai0 === 4) {
437	    const numBolt = ftw ? 60 : 80;
438	    if (isReal && e.ai1 >= 20 && (e.ai1 - 20) % numBolt === 0) {
439	      if (e.ai1 === 20) {
440	        cloneSupplement(game, cultistClones(game, e), player, 1, 0.5235987901687622);
441	        // 原版闪电发射零吼(同上全段普查)——曾借 roar 0.5,2026-08-18 对账删
442	      }
443	      // 原版 465 零速悬停后从天引雷(Projectile.AI 收敛:直飞 6 速朝玩家)
444	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - (e.cy - 100);
445	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
446	      shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy - 100, dx / d * 6, dy / d * 6, 45, 465); // :65563-65566
447	    }
448	    e.ai1 += 1;
449	    if (e.ai1 >= 20 + numBolt) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; if (isReal) e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
450	    return;
451	  }
452	
453	  // ---- ai0 == 5 仪式瞬移(:65623-65770):0-30 淡出,30 环位重定位 + 生成/补位克隆体,
454	  //      30-90 隐形,90-120 淡入,120-420 显形可受击(打真身/克隆体窗口);持续 420 ----
455	  if (e.ai0 === 5) {
456	    const t = e.ai1;
457	    if (t >= 0 && t < 30) {
458	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.floor((t / 30) * 254);
459	      e.iframes = 2; // flag3/flag4
460	    } else if (t >= 30 && t < 90) {
461	      e.spawnAlpha = 254;
462	      e.iframes = 2;
463	      if (t === 30 && isReal) {
464	        // :65636-65675:环绕自身半径 180 的 num29 环位重定位——
465	        //   补位克隆体(总数目标 6,每轮至多新造 2),余下槽位重排既有克隆体;
466	        //   真身取「距玩家最远」槽(num31 = 最近槽的对侧);圈心(=重定位前中心)存
467	        //   la2 供本态朝向用,并于此原位发 490 仪式圈弹（:65643，ai[1]=真身索引）
468	        la.la1 += 1; // localAI[1] 仪式代数(:65637)
469	        la.la2 = { x: e.cx, y: e.cy };
470	        (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (en: Entity, b: string) => void } }).entities
471	          .add(new CultistRitualCircle(e), 'projectiles');
472	        const clones = cultistClones(game, e);
473	        let num28 = 6 - clones.length; // :65643-65647
474	        if (num28 > 2) num28 = 2;
475	        const num29 = clones.length + num28 + 1;
476	        const dists: number[] = [];
477	        for (let s = 0; s < num29; s++) { // :65650-65656 各槽到玩家距离
478	          const [rx, ry] = rot(180, 0, (s * Math.PI * 2) / num29 - Math.PI / 2);
479	          dists.push(Math.hypot(e.cx + rx - player.cx, e.cy + ry - player.cy));
480	        }
481	        let num31 = 0; // :65658-65664 取最近槽 → 映射到对侧(最远)槽
482	        for (let s = 1; s < num29; s++) if (dists[num31] > dists[s]) num31 = s;
483	        num31 = num31 >= num29 / 2 ? num31 - Math.floor(num29 / 2) : num31 + Math.floor(num29 / 2);
484	        let num33 = num28; // :65666 新造配额,耗尽后按序重排既有克隆体(list6[-num33-1])
485	        let reused = 0;
486	        for (let s = 0; s < num29; s++) {
487	          if (num31 === s) continue;
488	          const [rx, ry] = rot(180, 0, (s * Math.PI * 2) / num29 - Math.PI / 2);
489	          const px = e.cx + rx, py = e.cy + ry;
490	          if (num33-- > 0) spawnCultistClone(game, e, px, py, la.la1);
491	          else if (reused < clones.length) {
492	            const c = clones[reused++];
493	            c.x = px - c.w / 2;
494	            c.y = py - c.h / 2;
495	          }
496	        }
497	        // 真身移到 num31 槽(:65652 Center += 偏移向量)
498	        const [fx, fy] = rot(180, 0, (num31 * Math.PI * 2) / num29 - Math.PI / 2);
499	        e.x += fx;
500	        e.y += fy;
501	        e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
502	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#8060D0', 18, 2.6);
503	        // 原版仪式圈就位零吼(同上全段普查)——曾借 roar 0.7,2026-08-18 对账删
504	      }
505	    } else if (t >= 90 && t < 120) {
506	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.floor(254 * (1 - (t - 90) / 30));
507	      e.iframes = 2;
508	    } else {
509	      // 120-420:显形且可受击(alpha=0,不置 iframes;chaseable 原版为 false,:65954)
510	      e.spawnAlpha = 0;
511	      // 朝向:以仪式圈心为基准(:65706-65719 / :65743-65756 sign(圈心-自身).X)
512	      const cir = ritualCenterOf(isReal, la, parent);
513	      if (cir) {
514	        const dx = cir.x - e.cx;
515	        if (dx !== 0) e.facing = Math.sign(dx);
516	      }
517	    }
518	    e.ai1 += 1;
519	    if (e.ai1 >= 420) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; if (isReal) e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
520	    return;
521	  }
522	
523	  // ---- ai0 == 6 硬直(:65771-65784):打中克隆体后真身暴露(不置 iframes),120t 后回选招 ----
524	  if (e.ai0 === 6) {
525	    e.ai1 += 1;
526	    if (e.ai1 >= 120) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; if (isReal) e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
527	    flyIntegrate(e);
528	    return;
529	  }
530	
531	  // ---- ai0 == 7 远古之光(:65796-65877):每 20t 一轮×2;第三拍克隆体各补 5 发 468
532	  //      (±1.2566rad,:65796-65822),真身每轮以扇面(步进 2π/25,8 速)召 5 只 NPC522
533	  //      (:65862-65865,ai1=逐 t 弯转角 → AI_100 轨道弹);持续 4+20*2 ----
534	  if (e.ai0 === 7) {
535	    if (isReal && e.ai1 >= 4 && (e.ai1 - 4) % 20 === 0) {
536	      e.facing = Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx) || e.facing; // :65839-65843
537	      const aim = aimLead(e, player, e.facing);
538	      const num47 = (Math.PI * 2) / 25; // :65856
539	      if ((e.ai1 - 4) / 20 === 2) {
540	        cloneSupplement(game, cultistClones(game, e), player, 5, 1.2566370964050293);
541	      }
542	      for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
543	        const [dvx, dvy] = rot(aim.x * 8, aim.y * 8, num47 * i - (Math.PI * 2 / 5 - num47) / 2); // :65860
544	        const n = spawnPart(game, e, 522);
545	        if (n) {
546	          // NewNPC(..., vector15.X, vector15.Y + 7, 522, ...) → X=中心横坐标,Y=底部纵坐标
547	          n.ai0 = 0;
548	          n.ai1 = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3 * (Math.PI * 2) / 60; // :65861 逐 t 弯转角
549	          n.ai2 = dvx; n.ai3 = dvy;                                  // 初速(AI_100 首拍取用)
550	          n.x = e.cx + e.facing * 30 - n.w / 2;
551	          n.y = e.cy + 12 + 7 - n.h;
552	          n.vx = dvx; n.vy = dvy;
553	        }
554	      }
555	      // 原版火球(522)发射零吼(同上全段普查)——曾借 roar 0.5,2026-08-18 对账删
556	    }
557	    e.ai1 += 1;
558	    if (e.ai1 >= 4 + 20 * 2) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; if (isReal) e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
559	    return;
560	  }
561	
562	  // ---- ai0 == 8 远古末日(:65873-65932):玩家附近随机空位撒 NPC523,数量 = min(克隆体+1, 3)
563	  //      (:65892-65897);经典选招表不含 6(expert 随机替换专属,:65270-65275),状态体保留 ----
564	  if (e.ai0 === 8) {
565	    if (isReal && e.ai1 >= 4 && (e.ai1 - 4) % 20 === 0) {
566	      const st = game.world.store;
567	      const ptx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(player.cy / TILE);
568	      const etx = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE), ety = Math.floor(e.cy / TILE);
569	      const want = Math.min(cultistClones(game, e).length + 1, 3);
570	      for (let k = 0; k < want; k++) {
571	        for (let tries = 0; tries < 100; tries++) { // :65904-65931 空位采样
572	          const tx = ptx + Math.floor(Math.random() * 41) - 20;
573	          const ty = pty + Math.floor(Math.random() * 41) - 20;
574	          const nearPlayer = Math.abs(ty - pty) <= 7 && Math.abs(tx - ptx) <= 7;
575	          const nearSelf = Math.abs(ty - ety) <= 3 && Math.abs(tx - etx) <= 3;
576	          if (nearPlayer || nearSelf || !st.inBounds(tx, ty) || st.isSolid(tx, ty)) continue;
577	          const n = spawnPart(game, e, 523);
578	          if (n) {
579	            n.x = tx * TILE + 8 - n.w / 2;
580	            n.y = ty * TILE + 8 - n.h / 2;
581	            n.vx = 0; n.vy = 0;
582	            n.ai0 = e.id; // :65930 ai[0] = 真身 whoAmI(本仓以运行时 id 代)
583	            n.bInit = true;
584	          }
585	          break;
586	        }
587	      }
588	    }
589	    e.ai1 += 1;
590	    if (e.ai1 >= 4 + 20 * 3) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; if (isReal) e.ai3 += 1; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0; }
591	    return;
592	  }
593	
594	  // 未知状态:回选招
595	  e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0;
596	}
597	
598	// ===================== AI_120 光之女皇 636(NPC.cs:46299-47302) =====================
599	// 状态机(ai0): 0 出生(180t) / 1 悬浮蓄力枢纽(DashTo 靠近 10t + 减速,45t(二阶段 20t)选招)/
600	//   2 彩虹射线 873 / 4 光枪墙 919 / 5 永恒彩虹 872 / 6 太阳舞 923 / 7 光枪回环 919(二阶段)/
601	//   8,9 刀阵冲刺(±50 速,6-40t 无敌蓄力,40-90t 伤害 ×1.5)/ 10 半血变身传送 /
602	//   13 淡出退场(目标失效/超 6400px)。3(874 天降极光)与 11/12 为 expert 专属拍,跳过。
603	// 经典弹幕伤害(:46326-46345): 919 墙 50 / 919 回环 70 / 873 45 / 872 45 / 923 50 / 874 40。
604	// 选招表(:46400-46490): 一阶段 10 拍 [2,8,6,8,5,2,8,4,8,5] 循环,血 ≤50% 强制 10(变身);
605	//   二阶段 9 拍 [7,2,8,5,2,6,6,4,8] 循环(expert 追加 11/12 拍已剔除)。
606	const EMPRESS_CYCLE_P1 = [2, 8, 6, 8, 5, 2, 8, 4, 8, 5];
607	const EMPRESS_CYCLE_P2 = [7, 2, 8, 5, 2, 6, 6, 4, 8];
608	
609	/** 原版 AI_120_HallowBoss_DashTo(:47304-47317):目标上移 300,>200 再回退 100,
610	 *  速度 = lerp(单位向量*min(len,18), vector/6, lerpValue(100,600,len)) */
611	function empressDashTo(e: Enemy, tx0: number, ty0: number): void {
612	  let tx = tx0, ty = ty0 - 300;
613	  const d0 = Math.hypot(tx - e.cx, ty - e.cy);
614	  if (d0 > 200) {
615	    const ux = (tx - e.cx) / (d0 || 1), uy = (ty - e.cy) / (d0 || 1);
616	    tx -= ux * 100; ty -= uy * 100;
617	  }
618	  const vx = tx - e.cx, vy = ty - e.cy;
619	  const len = Math.hypot(vx, vy) || 1;
620	  const lv = lerpValue(100, 600, len);
621	  const cap = Math.min(len, 18);
622	  e.vx = vLerp((vx / len) * cap, vx / 6, lv);
623	  e.vy = vLerp((vy / len) * cap, vy / 6, lv);
624	}
625	
626	/** 悬浮趋近(:46502 系):距 (玩家+偏移) > 40 才以 SimpleFlyMovement(dir*12, 0.5) 靠近 */
627	function empressHover(e: Enemy, player: Player, ox: number, oy: number, spdMul = 1, accMul = 1): void {
628	  const tx = player.cx + ox, ty = player.cy + oy;
629	  if (Math.hypot(tx - e.cx, ty - e.cy) > 40) {
630	    const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
631	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
632	    simpleFly(e, (dx / d) * 12 * spdMul, (dy / d) * 12 * spdMul, 0.5 * accMul);
633	  }
634	}
635	
636	export function empressOfLightAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): void {
637	  if (!e.bInit) {
638	    e.bInit = true;
639	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
640	    e.spawnAlpha = 254; // Opacity = ai1/180 渐显(:46410)
641	    game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy - 80, '#FFE080', 20, 2.5);
642	  }
643	  const phase2 = e.ai3 === 1 || e.ai3 === 3; // AI_120_HallowBoss_IsInPhase2 :46179
644	  const b = baseOf(e);
645	  // :47296-47301 二阶段防御 ×1.2;白天暴怒（flag4,:47296 段 damage=9999——wiki"白天必秒杀"）
646	  // ——2026-08-13 接通:白天召唤女皇不再无风险
647	  const dayEnrage = game.world.clock.isDay;
648	  // :46319-46323 满血+白天且未暴怒 → ai3 += 2（白天召出即 Enraged 档 2;
649	  // 受过伤再转白天不补——原版"只在白天受过伤"语义）。NpcDrops 的
650	  // EmpressOfLightIsGenuinelyEnraged(:46169)读 ai3∈{2,3} → 夜光 5005 掉落门
651	  if (e.hp === e.maxHp && dayEnrage && e.ai3 !== 2 && e.ai3 !== 3) e.ai3 += 2;
652	  e.def.damage = dayEnrage ? 9999 : b.dmg;
653	  e.def.defense = Math.floor(phase2 ? b.def * 1.2 : b.def);
654	
655	  // 目标失效/超 6400px → 淡出退场(:46437-46444/:46595 num36=13;此后只有 13 态不触 player)
656	  // 真狂暴入夜强制离场（:46580-46595：IsGenuinelyEnraged(ai3∈{2,3}) 且 !dayTime →
657	  // 选招强制 13 态淡出——白天召出的女皇入夜即飞走,2026-08-18 审查补）
658	  const nightLeave = (e.ai3 === 2 || e.ai3 === 3) && !dayEnrage;
659	  if ((!player || player.dead || Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > 6400 || nightLeave) && e.ai0 !== 13) {
660	    e.ai0 = 13; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 += 1;
661	    e.vx /= 4; e.vy /= 4;
662	  }
663	  if (player) e.facing = e.seekDirX(player);
664	  if (!player) {
665	    // 目标彻底失效:上方已强制 ai0=13,此处推进淡出自毁(:47236/:47264-47271)
666	    e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(254, e.spawnAlpha + 5);
667	    e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;
668	    e.ai1 += 1;
669	    flyIntegrate(e);
670	    if (e.ai1 >= 20 && e.spawnAlpha >= 254) e.dead = true;
671	    return;
672	  }
673	
674	  // 冲刺窗口伤害倍率(num16,:46352/:47006):8/9 态 40-90t 时 ×1.5
675	  // （白天 9999 基伤同样参与 ×1.5——原版 flag4 先把 num 全置 9999 再乘 num16;
676	  //  曾按 b.dmg 重算把 9999 整档覆盖掉,白天暴怒伤害从未生效）
677	  const dashBoost = (e.ai0 === 8 || e.ai0 === 9) && e.ai1 > 40 && e.ai1 <= 90;
678	  // :47294-47299 damage = def×num16 后 flag4 **直接覆写 9999**（不乘 1.5）——
679	  //  曾得 9999×1.5=14999（乘区序错，2026-08-18 审查勘误）
680	  e.def.damage = dayEnrage ? 9999 : Math.round(b.dmg * (dashBoost ? 1.5 : 1));
681	
682	  switch (e.ai0) {
683	    // ---- 0 出生(:46370-46422):velocity=(0,5) 后 0.95 阻尼,180t 渐显;全程无敌(flag6=false) ----
684	    case 0: {
685	      if (e.ai1 === 0) { e.vx = 0; e.vy = 5; }
686	      e.iframes = 2; // :46408-46409 flag6=false → dontTakeDamage
687	      e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;
688	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, 254 - Math.floor(clamp(e.ai1 / 180, 0, 1) * 254));
689	      e.ai1 += 1;
690	      if (e.ai1 >= 180) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
691	      break;
692	    }
693	    // ---- 1 悬浮蓄力枢纽(:46418-46511):前 10t DashTo 冲近,之后阻尼;到时按表选招 ----
694	    case 1: {
695	      // num34 :46422(flag=二阶段 20/一阶段 45);getGoodWorld 折半(:46425-46428)
696	      let dur = phase2 ? 20 : 45;
697	      if (game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld) dur /= 2;
698	      if (e.ai1 <= 10) empressDashTo(e, player.cx, player.cy);
699	      if (Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy) > 16 && e.ai1 > 10) { e.vx /= 2; e.vy /= 2; }
700	      e.vx *= 0.92; e.vy *= 0.92;
701	      e.ai1 += 1;
702	      if (e.ai1 < dur) break;
703	      const table = phase2 ? EMPRESS_CYCLE_P2 : EMPRESS_CYCLE_P1;
704	      let next = table[Math.floor(e.ai2) % table.length];
705	      if (!phase2 && e.hp / e.maxHp <= 0.5) next = 10; // :46463 半血强制变身
706	      if (next === 8 && player.cx > e.cx) next = 9;     // :46493 冲刺方向
707	      e.ai0 = next; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 += 1;
708	      break;
709	    }
710	    // ---- 2 彩虹射线 873(:46512-46649):悬玩家左上(-150,-250),每 3t 一发直飞,60t 止 ----
711	    case 2: {
712	      empressHover(e, player, -150, -250);
713	      const dur = 60 + (90 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0)); // num90 - num17 :46517/:46356
714	      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 3 === 0 && e.ai1 < 60) {
715	        const [dvx, dvy] = rot(0, -6, (Math.PI / 2) * (Math.random() * 2 - 1));
716	        shoot(game, e.cx - 55, e.cy - 30, dvx, dvy, dayEnrage ? 9999 : 45, 873);
717	      }
718	      e.ai1 += 1;
719	      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
720	      break;
721	    }
722	    // ---- 4 光枪墙 919(:46695-46779):悬玩家上 (0,-350),每 4t 在玩家周围 300px 环上布枪 ----
723	    case 4: {
724	      empressHover(e, player, 0, -350);
725	      const dur = 100 + (20 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0));
726	      // :46715 每 4t 布一杆;:46734 距玩家 >2400 跳过当拍(原版 continue,计时不断)
727	      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 4 === 0 && e.ai1 < 100 && Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) <= 2400) {
728	        const num82 = 4; // 经典枪数(expert 5)
729	        const num85 = Math.floor(e.ai1) / 4;
730	        let ux = Math.cos(Math.PI / (num82 * 2) + num85 * (Math.PI / num82));
731	        let uy = Math.sin(Math.PI / (num82 * 2) + num85 * (Math.PI / num82));
732	        ux += ux > 0 ? 0.5 : -0.5; // 经典横向外扩(:46725-46727)
733	        const ul = Math.hypot(ux, uy) || 1; ux /= ul; uy /= ul;
734	        if (player.vx * ux + player.vy * uy > 0) { ux = -ux; uy = -uy; } // 逆着玩家速度布枪(:46738)
735	        const leadX = player.cx + player.vx * 90, leadY = player.cy + player.vy * 90;
736	        let px = player.cx + ux * 300 - player.vx * 30;
737	        let py = player.cy + uy * 300 - player.vy * 30;
738	        const pd = Math.hypot(px - player.cx, py - player.cy);
739	        if (pd < 300) { // 环距不足外推(:46724-46731)
740	          const ex = player.cx - px, ey = player.cy - py;
741	          const el = Math.hypot(ex, ey) || 1;
742	          px = player.cx - (ex / el) * 300; py = player.cy - (ey / el) * 300;
743	        }
744	        const ax = leadX - px, ay = leadY - py;
745	        const al = Math.hypot(ax, ay) || 1;
746	        // 原版 919 零速预告后沿 v4 方向冲刺(Projectile.AI 收敛:12 速直飞)
747	        shoot(game, px, py, (ax / al) * 12, (ay / al) * 12, dayEnrage ? 9999 : 50, 919, 240);
748	      }
749	      e.ai1 += 1;
750	      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
751	      break;
752	    }
753	    // ---- 5 永恒彩虹 872(:46780-46870):悬玩家上,开场 13 发环形彩弹(8 速) ----
754	    case 5: {
755	      empressHover(e, player, 0, -350);
756	      const dur = 42 + (30 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0));
757	      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 42 === 0 && e.ai1 < 42) {
758	        const base = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
759	        for (let k = 0; k < 13; k++) {
760	          const t = k / 13;
761	          const [ux, uy] = rot(0, 1, Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI * 2 * t + base);
762	          const [ox, oy] = rot(ux, uy, -Math.PI / 2);
763	          shoot(game, e.cx + 55 + ox * 30, e.cy - 30 + oy * 30, ux * 8, uy * 8, dayEnrage ? 9999 : 45, 872);
764	        }
765	        game.playSfxWav?.('Item_163', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :46805 彩虹发射(曾借 roar 0.4)
766	      }
767	      e.ai1 += 1;
768	      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
769	      break;
770	    }
771	    // ---- 6 太阳舞 923(:46871-46935):悬玩家上 (-80,-500) 低速,每 60t 一环 6 发,3 环 ----
772	    case 6: {
773	      empressHover(e, player, -80, -500, 0.3, 0.7);
774	      const dur = 180 + (120 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0));
775	      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 60 === 0 && e.ai1 < 180) {
776	        const volley = Math.floor(e.ai1) / 60;
777	        const flip = player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : 0;
778	        for (let t = 0; t < 1; t += 1 / 6) {
779	          const slot = (t + (1 / 6) * 0.5 + volley * (1 / 6) * 0.5) % 1;
780	          const a = Math.PI * 2 * (slot + flip);
781	          // 原版 923 零速绕体公转(收敛:8 速放射)
782	          shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy - 100, Math.cos(a) * 8, Math.sin(a) * 8, dayEnrage ? 9999 : 50, 923, 240);
783	        }
784	      }
785	      e.ai1 += 1;
786	      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
787	      break;
788	    }
789	    // ---- 7 光枪回环 919(二阶段,:46936-47049):每 60t 以玩家为中心布 13 连枪线,4 线 ----
790	    case 7: {
791	      empressHover(e, player, 0, -350, 0.4, 1);
792	      const dur = 240 + (20 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0));
793	      if (Math.floor(e.ai1) % 60 === 0 && e.ai1 < 240) {
794	        const line = Math.floor(e.ai1) / 60;
795	        let cx0 = player.cx, cy0 = player.cy;
796	        let dirX = 0, dirY = 0, axX = 0, axY = 0;
797	        const span = 1950; // num76 = 13*150 :46961
798	        switch (line) { // :46976-47011 四线几何
799	          case 0: cx0 += -span / 2; dirX = 0; dirY = span; axX = 1; axY = 0; break;
800	          case 1: cx0 += span / 2; cy0 += 75; dirX = 0; dirY = span; axX = -1; axY = 0; break;
801	          case 2: cx0 += -span * 0.4; cy0 += -span * 0.4; dirX = span * 1.4; dirY = 0; axX = 1; axY = 1; break;
802	          default: cx0 += span * 0.4 + 75; cy0 += -span * 0.4; dirX = -span * 1.4; dirY = 0; axX = -1; axY = 1; break;
803	        }
804	        const al = Math.hypot(axX, axY) || 1;
805	        for (let t = 0; t <= 1.0001; t += 1 / 13) {
806	          const ox = cx0 + dirX * (t - 0.5), oy = cy0 + dirY * (t - 0.5);
807	          shoot(game, ox, oy, (axX / al) * 12, (axY / al) * 12, dayEnrage ? 9999 : 70, 919, 240);
808	        }
809	        game.playSfxWav?.('Item_160', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :46997 四线发射(曾借 roar 0.45)
810	      }
811	      e.ai1 += 1;
812	      if (e.ai1 >= dur) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
813	      break;
814	    }
815	    // ---- 8/9 刀阵冲刺(:47053-47089):0-40t 冲到玩家侧后 550px(6-40t 无敌),
816	    //      40-90t 以 ±50 横速突进(伤害 ×1.5),其后 0.92 阻尼 ----
817	    case 8:
818	    case 9: {
819	      const side = e.ai0 === 8 ? -1 : 1; // num33 :47056
820	      if (e.ai1 <= 40) {
821	        if (e.ai1 >= 6) e.iframes = 2; // flag6 → dontTakeDamage(:47058)
822	        const destX = player.cx + side * -550, destY = player.cy;
823	        const dx = destX - e.cx, dy = destY - e.cy;
824	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
825	        simpleFly(e, (dx / d) * 12, (dy / d) * 12, 1.0);
826	        if (e.ai1 === 40) { e.vx *= 0.3; e.vy *= 0.3; }
827	      } else if (e.ai1 <= 90) {
828	        e.vx = vLerp(side * 50, e.vx, 0.05);
829	        e.vy = vLerp(0, e.vy, 0.05);
830	        if (e.ai1 === 90) { e.vx *= 0.7; e.vy *= 0.7; }
831	      } else {
832	        e.vx *= 0.92; e.vy *= 0.92;
833	      }
834	      e.ai1 += 1;
835	      if (e.ai1 >= 90 + (20 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0))) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; }
836	      break;
837	    }
838	    // ---- 10 半血变身(:47106-47135):90t 时传送玩家上 250 处并 ai3 0→1(进入二阶段) ----
839	    case 10: {
840	      if (e.ai1 >= 30 && e.ai1 <= 170) e.iframes = 2; // flag6 → dontTakeDamage(:47110)
841	      if (e.ai1 === 0) game.playSfxFiles?.(['Item_161'], 1, e.cx, e.cy); // :47036 状态开始音（仅此一次）
842	      e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;
843	      if (e.ai1 === 90) {
844	        if (e.ai3 === 0) e.ai3 = 1; // :47040-47043
845	        if (e.ai3 === 2) e.ai3 = 3; // :47044-47047 白天暴怒档同步进二阶段(3=暴怒+二阶段)
846	        e.x = player.cx - e.w / 2;
847	        e.y = player.cy - 250 - e.h / 2;
848	        // 原版此传送为纯 position 改写（:47039-47052）：无 dust 无传送音——
849	        // 曾错加 roar+黄尘（已移除对齐）
850	      }
851	      e.ai1 += 1;
852	      if (e.ai1 >= 180 + (20 - (phase2 ? 15 : 0))) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
853	      break;
854	    }
855	    // ---- 13 淡出退场(:47226-47281):alpha 渐隐,全隐后移除 ----
856	    case 13: {
857	      e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(254, e.spawnAlpha + 5);
858	      e.ai1 += 1;
859	      if (e.ai1 >= 20 && e.spawnAlpha >= 254) e.dead = true;
860	      break;
861	    }
862	    default:
863	      e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0;
864	      break;
865	  }
866	  // :47292 alpha 渐隐回落(0/13 态各自管控 spawnAlpha,不参与)
867	  if (e.ai0 !== 0 && e.ai0 !== 13) e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 5);
868	  flyIntegrate(e);
869	
870	  // 点光(:47298-47302 AI_120 尾段)：白光 × Opacity（=1−alpha/255；本仓
871	  // spawnAlpha 254=全隐 → 0=全显，故 Opacity≈1−spawnAlpha/254）
872	  const o = 1 - e.spawnAlpha / 254;
873	  e.lightRGB = [o, o, o];
874	}
875	
876	// ===================== AI_121 史莱姆皇后 657(NPC.cs:45543-46177) =====================
877	// 状态机(ai0): 0 待机(一阶段地面/二阶段 FlyMovement) / 1 传送后显形 / 2 传送消散 /
878	//   3 走路连跳(-8,-8,-6,-13 四连) / 4 升空砸地(ai2=0 升空 60t → ai2=1 重力坠,落地撒 922) /
879	//   5 凝胶环(ai2=0 蓄 50t → ai2=1 撒 926 环)。
880	// 半血(flag2)进二阶段:悬浮 FlyMovement + 攻速变化 + 暴怒(离开神圣之地)分支按任务跳过。
881	// 经典数值(:45546-45552): 砸地 922 40 伤 / 凝胶环 926 30 伤;砸地蓄力 30t(二阶段 10t);
882	//   凝胶环 6 发(二阶段 10 发);仆从按掉血 2%(二阶段 1.5%)撒 1-2 只 NPC 658/659/660。
883	const QUEEN_MINION_VANILLA = [658, 659, 660]; // vanilla :46134-46146 蓝/粉/紫仆从
884	// 数据核对(1.4.5.6):658/659/660 已在 public/sprites/vanilla-npcs.json,与 SetDefaults
885	//   :17261-17284 逐字段一致(658/659 QueenSlimeMinion 24×18 aiStyle1 伤40 防35 血150;
886	//   660 QueenSlimeMinionPurple 40×30 aiStyle14 伤50 防30 血120),贴图 NPC_658/659/660.png
887	//   亦在 sprites/vanilla —— 真身直连,aiStyle 1/14 均已接线。
888	// FALLBACK 仅作数据文件缺失时的防御(535 刺史莱姆/537 沙史莱姆/16 蓝史莱姆)。
889	const QUEEN_MINION_FALLBACK = [535, 537, 16];
890	
891	/** AI_015_KingSlime_FindTeleportSpot(NPC.cs:43402-43434)收敛:anti → 玩家脚下;
892	 *  否则玩家 10 格半径内找"脚下实心 + 头顶 7 格开阔"的随机格,Bottom 落点返回(:43417-43418) */
893	function queenTeleportSpot(game: GameHooks, player: Player, anti: boolean): { x: number; y: number; found: boolean } {
894	  if (anti) return { x: player.x + player.w / 2, y: player.y + player.h, found: false }; // :43406-43409
895	  const st = game.world.store;
896	  const ptx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(player.cy / TILE);
897	  const cands: Array<[number, number]> = [];
898	  for (let x = ptx - 10; x <= ptx + 10; x++) {
899	    for (let y = pty - 10; y <= pty + 10; y++) {
900	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !st.isSolid(x, y)) continue;
901	      let clear = true;
902	      for (let k = 1; k <= 7; k++) {                       // 头顶 7 格(BuildCache 10/7 参数)
903	        if (st.inBounds(x, y - k) && st.isSolid(x, y - k)) { clear = false; break; }
904	      }
905	      if (clear) cands.push([x, y]);
906	    }
907	  }
908	  if (cands.length > 0) {
909	    const [x, y] = cands[Math.floor(Math.random() * cands.length)];
910	    return { x: x * TILE + TILE / 2, y: y * TILE, found: true };
911	  }
912	  return { x: player.x + player.w / 2, y: player.y + player.h, found: false }; // :43430-43433 兜底
913	}
914	
915	/** AI_121_QueenSlime_FlyMovement(:45467-45542):12 速/0.085 加速度趋近玩家上方 250,
916	 *  反向且 >100px 时加速度 ×2;旋转 = vx*0.1 钳 ±0.5 */
917	function queenFlyMovement(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player): void {
918	  e.onGround = false;
919	  const spd = 12, accBase = 0.085, hover = 250;
920	  let tx = player.cx, ty = player.cy - hover;
921	  if (losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) {
922	    tx = player.cx; ty = player.cy - hover;
923	  } else {
924	    // :45478-45496 无视线 → 逐级上探玩家上方第一个实心格下方(简化:玩家上方 16px 一档,探 16 档)
925	    const st = game.world.store;
926	    let found = false;
927	    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
928	      const py = player.cy - 16 * i;
929	      const ptx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
930	      if (st.inBounds(ptx, pty) && st.isSolid(ptx, pty)) {
931	        tx = player.cx; ty = py + 16;
932	        found = true;
933	        break;
934	      }
935	    }
936	    if (!found) { tx = player.cx; ty = player.cy; }
937	  }
938	  let dvx = tx - e.cx, dvy = ty - e.cy;
939	  const dist = Math.hypot(dvx, dvy);
940	  let acc = accBase;
941	  if (Math.abs(dvx) < 40) dvx = e.vx;                       // :45512-45514
942	  const lim = spd - 2;
943	  if (dist > 100 && ((e.vx < -lim && dvx > 0) || (e.vx > lim && dvx < 0))) acc *= 2; // :45516-45519
944	  if (dist < 40) { dvx = e.vx; dvy = e.vy; }                // :45521-45522
945	  else if (dist < 80) { dvx = (dvx / (dist || 1)) * spd * 0.65; dvy = (dvy / (dist || 1)) * spd * 0.65; }
946	  else { dvx = (dvx / (dist || 1)) * spd; dvy = (dvy / (dist || 1)) * spd; }
947	  simpleFly(e, dvx, dvy, acc);
948	  e.spin = clamp(e.vx * 0.1, -0.5, 0.5); // :45536-45541
949	}
950	
951	export function queenSlimeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): void {
952	  const la = laOf(e);
953	  if (!e.bInit) {
954	    // :45559-45566 localAI[0]==0:ai[1]=-100,localAI[0]=lifeMax
955	    e.bInit = true;
956	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = -100; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
957	    la.la0 = e.maxHp;
958	  }
959	  const phase2 = e.hp <= e.maxHp / 2; // flag2 :45552
960	  // 点光(:45557-45558 AI_121 头段,恒亮无门)：Center (1,0.7,0.9) 粉光
961	  e.lightRGB = [1, 0.7, 0.9];
962	  // 掉血阈值撒仆从(:46147-46177):localAI[0] 记上次标记血量,差值超 2%(二阶段 1.5%)即撒
963	  if (la.la0 >= e.maxHp / 2 && e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) {
964	    la.la0 = e.hp;            // :46142-46148 进二阶段复位状态
965	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
966	  }
967	  const b = baseOf(e);
968	  e.def.damage = b.dmg;
969	  e.def.defense = b.def;
970	  // 玩家死/超 500 格 → 鼓励离场(EncourageDespawn:45576;简化为远距上浮,不清除)
971	  if (!player || player.dead || Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) / 16 > 500) {
972	    e.encourageDespawn(10); // :45574 二次判定后仍失败 → EncourageDespawn(10)
973	    if (player) e.facing = player.cx < e.cx ? 1 : -1; // 背对玩家（:45576-45582）
974	    e.vy -= 0.06;
975	    e.y += e.vy; e.x += e.vx;
976	    e.spin = clamp(e.vx * 0.1, -0.5, 0.5);
977	    return;
978	  }
979	  const dirToPlayer = player.cx >= e.cx ? 1 : -1;
980	  e.facing = dirToPlayer;
981	
982	  // 传送蓄能(ai3,一阶段专属 :45576-45618):无视线或高差 >320 时 +1.5/t,否则 -1/t;
983	  // 满 300 且落地待机 → 进传送(2)。落点 = AI_015_KingSlime_FindTeleportSpot(NPC.cs:43402)
984	  // 收敛:玩家 10 格半径内"脚下实心+头顶 7 格开阔"的随机格(:43412 BuildCache 10/7 参数);
985	  // 反挂机(蓄满 360 或距玩家 >2000)→ 直接落到玩家脚下(:43404-43410)。
986	  if (!phase2) {
987	    const blocked = !losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy) || Math.abs(e.y - (player.y + player.h)) > 320;
988	    if (blocked) e.ai3 += 1.5;
989	    else e.ai3 = Math.max(0, e.ai3 - 1);
990	    if (e.ai3 >= 300 && e.ai0 === 0 && e.vy === 0 && e.onGround) {
991	      const anti = e.ai3 >= 360 || Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > 2000; // :45588-45595
992	      if (e.ai3 >= 360) e.ai3 = 360;      // :45591-45593
993	      const spot = queenTeleportSpot(game, player, anti);
994	      la.la1 = spot.x; la.la2 = spot.y;
995	      if (anti || spot.found) { e.ai0 = 2; e.ai1 = 0; }
996	      else e.ai3 = 0;                     // :45596-45599 找不到落点 → 重攒
997	    }
998	  }
999	
1000	  // noGravity/noTileCollide 置位(:45634-45635 默认关;case 4/5 与二阶段飞行开启)——
1001	  // 原版 case 4 整段(含 ai2=0 升空)置 noGravity,故一阶段升空同样免重力
1002	  const flying = e.ai0 === 5 || (e.ai0 === 4 && e.ai2 === 0) || (phase2 && e.ai0 === 0);
1003	  if (!flying) {
1004	    // 通用重力(NPC.cs:91519-91524,!noGravity 时 +0.3 钳 maxFallSpeed 10;
1005	    // 俯冲段(:45945)自身 +1 钳 14 与此叠加,实际仍受 10 上限——与原版一致)
1006	    if (!e.onGround) e.vy += 0.3;
1007	    if (e.vy > 10) e.vy = 10;
1008	  } else {
1009	    e.onGround = false;
1010	  }
1011	
1012	  switch (e.ai0) {
1013	    // ---- 0 待机(:45628-45686):地面摩擦 / 二阶段飞行;ai1 过阈值(60/120)选下一招 ----
1014	    case 0: {
1015	      if (phase2) {
1016	        queenFlyMovement(e, game, player);
1017	      } else if (e.onGround) {
1018	        e.vx *= 0.8;
1019	        if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
1020	        e.spin = 0;
1021	      }
1022	      // :45673 一阶段空中(!flag2 && velocity.Y!=0)不计数,落地后才推进选拍
1023	      if (!phase2 && !e.onGround && e.vy !== 0) break;
1024	      e.ai1 += 1;
1025	      const idle = phase2 ? 120 : 60; // num10 :45648-45652
1026	      if (e.ai1 > idle) {
1027	        e.ai1 = 0;
1028	        if (phase2) {
1029	          // :45660-45678 二阶段:默认砸地(ai2=1),玩家在上方或横距 >250 改凝胶环
1030	          let next = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 4 : 5;
1031	          let sub = next === 4 ? 1 : 0;
1032	          if (next === 4 && (player.y + player.h < e.y + e.h || Math.abs(player.cx - e.cx) > 250)) {
1033	            next = 5; sub = 0;
1034	          }
1035	          e.ai0 = next; e.ai2 = sub;
1036	        } else {
1037	          // :45680-45686 一阶段:随机 跳(3)/砸地(4)/凝胶环(5)
1038	          const r = Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
1039	          e.ai0 = r === 0 ? 3 : r === 1 ? 4 : 5;
1040	          e.ai2 = 0;
1041	        }
1042	      }
1043	      break;
1044	    }
1045	    // ---- 1 传送后显形(:45689-45720):30t;缩放动画(num3)为纯视觉,跳过 ----
1046	    case 1: {
1047	      e.spin = 0;
1048	      // 到达每帧 10 尘 4（:45747-45754）：GetDustColor=粉蓝 lerp（近似两色抽样）
1049	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, Math.random() < 0.5 ? '#50A0FF' : '#E060E0', 10, 2.4, { life: 30 });
1050	      e.ai1 += 1;
1051	      if (e.ai1 >= 30) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }
1052	      break;
1053	    }
1054	    // ---- 2 传送消散(:45721-45758):60t 后落位到 la1/la2(底部对齐) ----
1055	    case 2: {
1056	      e.spin = 0;
1057	      // 离场每帧 10 尘 4 ×0.5（:45780-45787）
1058	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, Math.random() < 0.5 ? '#50A0FF' : '#E060E0', 10, 1.2, { life: 30 });
1059	      e.ai1 += 1;
1060	      if (e.ai1 >= 60 && la.la1 !== 0) {
1061	        e.iframes = 2; // dontTakeDamage = hide(:46138)
1062	        e.x = la.la1 - e.w / 2;
1063	        e.y = (la.la2 as number) - e.h;
1064	        e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
1065	        e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0;
1066	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#7040A0', 16, 2.4);
1067	      } else if (e.ai1 > 90) {
1068	        e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; // 找不到落点的兜底
1069	      }
1070	      break;
1071	    }
1072	    // ---- 3 走路连跳(:45759-45844):四连跳 vy=-8/-8/-6/-13,横速递增 4/4.5/4/3.5;
1073	    //      落地重跳倒计时 ai1(血量 <66%/<33% 各 +4/t 加速) ----
1074	    case 3: {
1075	      e.spin = 0;
1076	      if (e.onGround) {
1077	        e.vx *= 0.8;
1078	        if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
1079	        e.ai1 += 4;
1080	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.66) e.ai1 += 4;
1081	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.33) e.ai1 += 4;
1082	        if (e.ai1 >= 0) {
1083	          if (e.ai2 === 3) {
1084	            e.vy = -13; e.vx += 3.5 * dirToPlayer;
1085	            e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
1086	            e.ai0 = 0; // 大跳结束回待机
1087	            game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.y + e.h, '#8060C0', 10, 2.2);
1088	          } else if (e.ai2 === 2) {
1089	            e.vy = -6; e.vx += 4.5 * dirToPlayer;
1090	            e.ai1 = -40; e.ai2 += 1;
1091	          } else {
1092	            e.vy = -8; e.vx += 4 * dirToPlayer;
1093	            e.ai1 = -40; e.ai2 += 1;
1094	          }
1095	          e.onGround = false;
1096	        }
1097	      } else {
1098	        // 空中横移(:45819-45844):朝玩家 0.2/t,上限 3(FTW 7,:45853-45857;
1099	        // 反向时 0.93 阻尼)
1100	        const cap = game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld ? 7 : 3;
1101	        if ((dirToPlayer === 1 && e.vx < cap) || (dirToPlayer === -1 && e.vx > -cap)) {
1102	          if ((dirToPlayer === -1 && e.vx < 0.1) || (dirToPlayer === 1 && e.vx > -0.1)) {
1103	            e.vx += 0.2 * dirToPlayer;
1104	          } else {
1105	            e.vx *= 0.93;
1106	          }
1107	        }
1108	      }
1109	      break;
1110	    }
1111	    // ---- 4 升空砸地(:45845-46006):ai2=0 飞向玩家上 384(60t) → ai2=1 重力坠(蓄力 30/10t,
1112	    //      FTW 0=无蓄力直接坠,:45885-45889;vy+1/t 钳 14,FTW 双倍加速钳 15.99,
1113	    //      :45944-45953);落地撒 922 冲击波(40 伤)+ 尘暴,回待机 ----
1114	    case 4: {
1115	      e.spin *= 0.9;
1116	      if (e.ai2 === 1) {
1117	        const num13 = game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld ? 0 : phase2 ? 10 : 30; // :45856-45860
1118	        e.vx *= 0.8;
1119	        if (!e.onGround) {
1120	          // (首帧越过 num13 的原版蓄冲吼经对账证伪删除——:45856-45960 零 PlaySound)
1121	          e.ai1 += 1;
1122	          if (e.ai1 >= num13) {
1123	            e.vy += 1;
1124	            // FTW(:45947-45952):额外 +1/t、钳 15.99
1125	            if (game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld) {
1126	              e.vy += 1;
1127	              if (e.vy === 0) e.vy = 0.01;
1128	              if (e.vy >= 15.99) e.vy = 15.99;
1129	            } else {
1130	              if (e.vy === 0) e.vy = 0.01;
1131	              if (e.vy >= 14) e.vy = 14; // num17 = 14(经典)
1132	            }
1133	          } else {
1134	            e.vy *= 0.8;
1135	          }
1136	          if (phase2 && e.ai1 > num13 + 120) { // 二阶段超时兜底(:45931-45938)
1137	            e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.vy *= 0.8;
1138	          }
1139	        } else {
1140	          // 落地冲击(:45870-45890):922 冲击波 + 尘暴 + 回待机
1141	          shoot(game, e.cx, e.y + e.h, 0, 0, 40, 922, 240);
1142	          game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.y + e.h, '#B090E0', 20, 3);
1143	          game.playSfxWav?.('Item_167', 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :45902 落地冲击(曾借 roar 0.7)
1144	          e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
1145	        }
1146	      } else {
1147	        // ai2=0 升空(:45967-46005):前 30t 原地蓄势(:45972-45974),之后 20 速冲向
1148	        // 玩家上方 384(超 500 格用自身),满 60t 转 ai2=1 俯冲
1149	        e.ai1 += 1;
1150	        if (e.ai1 >= 30) {
1151	          let tx = e.cx, ty = e.cy - 384;
1152	          if (Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) / 16 <= 500) { tx = player.cx; ty = player.cy - 384; }
1153	          const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
1154	          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
1155	          if (Math.abs(e.vy) < 0.5) { // 原版判 velocity.Y == 0;浮点残留放宽半格
1156	            const l = d || 1;
1157	            e.vx = (dx / l) * 20; e.vy = (dy / l) * 20;
1158	          } else {
1159	            e.vy *= 0.95;
1160	          }
1161	          if (e.ai1 >= 60) {
1162	            e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 1;
1163	            e.vy = -3; // :45982-45985
1164	            e.vx *= 0.5;
1165	          }
1166	        }
1167	      }
1168	      break;
1169	    }
1170	    // ---- 5 凝胶环(:46007-46121):ai2=0 蓄 50t(二阶段兼飞)→ ai2=1 第 10t 撒 926 环
1171	    //      (num6=10(FTW 15,:46026-46029)分度,num7 = 二阶段 num6 / 一阶段 6 发,
1172	    //      30 伤,9 速,-i*2π/num6) ----
1173	    case 5: {
1174	      e.spin *= 0.9;
1175	      if (e.ai2 === 1) {
1176	        e.ai1 += 1;
1177	        if (e.ai1 >= 10) {
1178	          const num6 = game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld ? 15 : 10;
1179	          const count = phase2 ? num6 : 6;
1180	          for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
1181	            const a = (-i * Math.PI * 2) / num6;
1182	            shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy, Math.cos(a) * 9, Math.sin(a) * 9, 30, 926);
1183	          }
1184	          // 原版凝胶环发射零吼(:46105 的 65 号声是 QueenSlime 1/360 环境声,
1185	          // 与发射时机无关)——曾借 roar 0.5,2026-08-18 对账删
1186	          e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
1187	        }

1246	  // 落地不停 → 永不弯转/自灭的直飞弹(F6 可直接造出,2026-08-13 ai0 清查)
1247	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; if (e.ai0 < 0) e.ai0 = 0; e.vx = e.ai2; e.vy = e.ai3; }
1248	  if (e.vy === 0 && e.ai0 >= 0) { e.ai0 = -1; e.ai1 = 0; return; }
1249	  if (e.ai0 === -1) {

1251	    e.ai1 += 1;
1252	    if (e.ai1 >= 5) e.dead = true;
1253	    return;
1254	  }

1279	  let rate = 1;
1280	  if (boss && boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 2) rate = 2;
1281	  if (boss && boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 4) rate = 3;
1282	  const gone = !boss || e.ai1 >= CHARGE_MAX;
1283	  e.ai1 += rate;
1284	  const prog = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, e.ai1 / 120)); // :42168 显形进度(前 120t)

1286	  e.spawnAlpha = Math.round(255 - prog * 255); // 原版 alpha 255→0
1287	  if (e.ai1 >= CHARGE_MAX) {
1288	    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
1289	      const ang = -Math.PI / 2 + (Math.PI / 2) * k;
1290	      shoot(game, e.cx, e.cy, Math.cos(ang) * 4, Math.sin(ang) * 4, e.def.damage, 593);
1291	    }
1292	  }
1293	  if (gone) e.dead = true;
1294	  void player;
1295	}

205	    else if (e.ai0 === 2) e.ai0 = -2;
206	  }
207	  if (e.ai0 === -1 || e.ai0 === -2) {
208	    e.vy -= 0.4;
209	    e.encourageDespawn(10);
210	    if (player && !player.dead) {
211	      e.despawnTimer = 300;                       // timeLeft = 300（:36377）
212	      if (e.ai0 === -2) e.ai0 = 2;
213	      else e.ai0 = 0;                             // 反编译此处为 if(ai0==0)ai0=0 无操作,
214	      e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;            // 按 -2→2 对称语义恢复巡航
215	    }
216	    fly(e);
217	    return;
218	  }
219	
220	  if (e.ai0 === 0) {
221	    // ---- 巡航攻击循环（:36390-36634）----
222	    const w1 = WIN(e.ai3);
223	    e.ai3 += 1;
224	    if (e.ai3 >= 600) e.ai3 = 0;
225	    const w = WIN(e.ai3);
226	    if (w !== w1) {                                // 窗口切换初始化（:36414-36430）
227	      if (w === 0) e.ai2 = 0;
228	      if (w === 1) e.ai2 = player && player.cx > center.x ? 1 : -1;
229	      if (w === 2) e.ai2 = 0;
230	    }
231	    const pcx = player ? player.cx : center.x, pcy = player ? player.cy : center.y;
232	    switch (w) {
233	      case 0: {
234	        // 远侧 600px 悬停（:36432-36446）：到点(<50px)跳 [19] 直进横掠
235	        if (e.ai2 === 0) e.ai2 = -600 * sign(center.x - pcx);
236	        let dx = pcx + e.ai2 - center.x, dy = pcy - 250 - center.y;
237	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
238	        if (d < 50) { e.ai3 = 19; break; }
239	        dx /= d; dy /= d;
240	        e.vx = lerp(e.vx, dx * 16, 0.1); e.vy = lerp(e.vy, dy * 16, 0.1);
241	        break;
242	      }
243	      case 1: {
244	        // 横掠（:36448-36480）：vx=3.5*ai[2];脚下净空<250px 上浮补齐
245	        const gap = groundGapPx(game, e);
246	        if (gap < 250) {
247	          const t = 4 > gap ? -gap : -4;
248	          e.vy = lerp(e.vy, t, 0.05);
249	        } else e.vy *= 0.95;
250	        e.vx = 3.5 * e.ai2;
251	        break;
252	      }
253	      case 2: {
254	        // 归位（:36514-36553）：玩家上方 ±300px;净空<170 抬升;到点(<70px)跳 [279] 进激光窗
255	        if (e.ai2 === 0) e.ai2 = 300 * sign(center.x - pcx);
256	        let dx = pcx + e.ai2 - center.x, dy = pcy - 170 - center.y;
257	        const gap = groundGapPx(game, e);
258	        if (gap < 170) dy -= 170 - gap;
259	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
260	        if (d < 70) { e.ai3 = 279; break; }
261	        dx /= d; dy /= d;
262	        e.vx = lerp(e.vx, dx * 20, 0.1); e.vy = lerp(e.vy, dy * 20, 0.1);
263	        break;
264	      }
265	      case 3: {
266	        // 激光窗缓漂（:36557-36582）：×0.85 双轴 + 净空<170 上浮
267	        const gap = groundGapPx(game, e);
268	        if (gap < 170) {
269	          const t = 4 > gap ? -gap : -4;
270	          e.vy = lerp(e.vy, t, 0.05);
271	        } else e.vy *= 0.85;
272	        e.vx *= 0.85;
273	        break;
274	      }
275	      case 4: {
276	        // 顶悬（:36591-36602）：到点(<50px)跳 [439] 进火炮窗
277	        let dx = pcx - center.x, dy = pcy - 250 - center.y;
278	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
279	        if (d < 50) { e.ai3 = 439; break; }
280	        dx /= d; dy /= d;
281	        e.vx = lerp(e.vx, dx * 16, 0.1); e.vy = lerp(e.vy, dy * 16, 0.1);
282	        break;
283	      }
284	      default: {
285	        // 火炮窗静止（:36604-36605）：×0.85 双轴
286	        e.vx *= 0.85; e.vy *= 0.85;
287	        break;
288	      }
289	    }
290	  } else if (e.ai0 === 1 && EXPERT) {
291	    // ---- expert 部件全灭旋转过渡（:36636-36668,150t）:本工程不可达 ----
292	    e.vx *= 0.96; e.vy *= 0.96;
293	    e.ai1 += 1;
294	    if (e.ai1 >= 150) { e.ai0 = 2; e.ai1 = 0; e.visAngle = 0; }
295	    else if (e.ai1 < 40) e.visAngle = Math.cos((e.ai1 / 40) * Math.PI * 2) * 0.2;
296	    else if (e.ai1 < 80) e.visAngle = Math.cos((e.ai1 / 20) * Math.PI * 2) * 0.3;
297	    else if (e.ai1 < 120) e.visAngle = Math.cos((e.ai1 / 10) * Math.PI * 2) * 0.4;
298	    else e.visAngle = ((e.ai1 - 120) / 30) * Math.PI * 2;
299	  } else if (e.ai0 === 2 && EXPERT) {
300	    // ---- expert 死光弹幕（:36670-36749,3600t 循环;每 120t 后 60t 为射线窗）----
301	    const wasWin = e.ai3 % 120 >= 60 ? 1 : 0;
302	    e.ai3 += 1;
303	    const inWin = e.ai3 % 120 >= 60 ? 1 : 0;
304	    if (inWin !== wasWin) {
305	      if (inWin === 1) {
306	        e.ai2 = player && player.cx > center.x ? 1 : -1;
307	        const ray = new MartianDeathray(e, 80);   // 447 死光电报（AI_079 实体）
308	        (game as unknown as { entities: { add(el: unknown, b: string): void } }).entities.add(ray, 'projectiles');
309	      }
310	    }
311	    if (e.ai3 >= 3600) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
312	    else if (inWin === 0 && player) {
313	      let dx = player.cx + e.ai2 * 350 - center.x, dy = player.cy - 250 - center.y;
314	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
315	      dx /= d; dy /= d;
316	      e.vx = lerp(e.vx, dx * 16, 0.1); e.vy = lerp(e.vy, dy * 16, 0.1);
317	    } else {
318	      const gap = groundGapPx(game, e);
319	      if (gap < 250) {
320	        const t = 4 > gap ? -gap : -4;
321	        e.vy = lerp(e.vy, t, 0.05);
322	      } else e.vy *= 0.95;
323	      e.vx = 8 * e.ai2;
324	    }
325	    e.visAngle = 0;
326	  }
327	  // ---- 出界钳制 + 消散（:36751-36772）----
328	  const st = game.world.store;
329	  if (e.y < -100 || e.x < -100 || e.y > st.h * TILE + 100 || e.x > st.w * TILE + 100) {
330	    e.x = clamp(e.x, -100, st.w * TILE + 100);
331	    e.y = clamp(e.y, -100, st.h * TILE + 100);
332	    e.dead = true;
333	    return;
334	  }
335	  fly(e);
336	}

338	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
339	// AI_075 飞碟部件 392/393/394 + 骑手 390（NPC.cs:35745-36321;416/492 同构锚表一并接）
340	// 锚点偏移（cs:35766/35813/35820/35827）:390→391 (0,-14);392→395 (0,2) 旋转随体;
341	//   393→395 (±60,+29);394→395 (±49,-13),spriteDirection=±1;416→415 (-spriteDir*10,-30)
342	// 主体消亡（:36288-36299）:390→Transform(382) / 416→Transform(518) / 其余消散
343	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
344	export function martianSaucerPartAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
345	  const id = e.vanillaId ?? 0;
346	  // ---- 荷兰大炮 492（DD2 批 bossAI_dd2.ts：锚船体偏移 cs:35889 + 240t 装填炮击
347	  // cs:36008）——aiStyle 75 分发进本函数,转发后不走飞碟部件路径 ----
348	  if (id === 492) { dutchmanCannonAI(e, game, player); return; }
349	  // ---- 骑手首帧生成坐骑（:35750-35762）----
350	  if ((id === 390 || id === 416) && !e.bInit) {
351	    e.bInit = true;
352	    const mount = spawnPart(game, e, id === 390 ? 391 : 415);
353	    if (mount) e.master = mount;
354	  }
355	  if (!e.bInit && (id === 392 || id === 393 || id === 394)) e.bInit = true;
356	
357	  const mount = e.master;
358	  const mountOk = !!mount && !mount.dead;
359	  if (!mountOk) {
360	    // 主体消亡分支（:36288-36299）
361	    if (id === 390 || id === 416) {
362	      e.transformTo(id === 390 ? 382 : 518, game.world.store, false, game);   // Transform → 下车变步兵
363	    } else {
364	      e.dead = true;   // 部件随主体消散（life=0 + active=false,无掉落）
365	    }
366	    return;
367	  }
368	  const m = mount as Enemy;
369	  // ---- 锚定跟随（:35764-35845 + 36200-36214）：零速贴主体 + 偏移 ----
370	  let ox = 0, oy = 0;
371	  let spriteDir = m.facing;
372	  if (id === 390) { ox = 0; oy = -14; }
373	  else if (id === 392) { ox = 0; oy = 2; }
374	  else if (id === 393) { ox = e.ai1 === 1 ? 60 : -60; oy = 29; }
375	  else if (id === 394) { ox = e.ai1 === 1 ? 49 : -49; oy = -13; spriteDir = e.ai1 === 1 ? 1 : -1; }
376	  else if (id === 416) { ox = -m.facing * 10; oy = -30; }
377	  e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
378	  e.x = m.cx - e.w / 2 + ox;
379	  e.y = m.cy - e.h / 2 + oy;
380	  // spriteDirection（394 按 ai[1] 固定侧,其余随主体;:36206-36212）
381	  e.facing = spriteDir === 1 ? 1 : -1;
382	
383	  if (id === 390) {
384	    // ---- 骑手射击（:36216-36280）：ai[1] 冷却 60t(受击 -30),<700px+LOS 朝向对 → 438 弹 ----
385	    e.vx = m.vx;
386	    if (e.ai1 < 60) e.ai1 += 1;
387	    if (e.justHit) e.ai1 = -30;
388	    if (player && !player.dead) {
389	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
390	      const canHit = losClear(game, e.x, e.y, player.x, player.y);
391	      if (canHit && Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 700) {
392	        if (e.ai1 === 60 && sign(dx) === e.facing) {
393	          e.ai1 = -60;
394	          const oxp = e.cx, oyp = e.cy - 4;
395	          let sx = player.cx - oxp, sy = player.cy - oyp;
396	          sx += rndRange(-50, 51); sy += rndRange(-50, 51);
397	          sx *= rndRange(80, 121) * 0.01; sy *= rndRange(80, 121) * 0.01;
398	          const d = Math.hypot(sx, sy);
399	          if (d > 0.001) { sx = (sx / d) * 7; sy = (sy / d) * 7; }
400	          else { sx = 0; sy = -7; }
401	          shootM(game, oxp, oyp, sx, sy, 30, 438);
402	          game.playSfxFiles?.(['Item_39'], 1, e.cx, e.cy);   // :36272 骑手(Scutlix 390)射弹音
403	        }
404	      }
405	    }
406	    return;
407	  }
408	  if (id !== 392 && id !== 393 && id !== 394) return;
409	
410	  // ---- 部件攻击窗（共享 saucer.ai[3] 计数,cs:36128/36171/36235）----
411	  const saucer = m;
412	  e.ai3 = saucer.ai3;
413	  if (id === 394) {
414	    // 火炮窗 [440,580)（:36124-36150）：每 20t 一发 448,朝向 ±22.5° 散布,速 8
415	    if (e.ai3 >= 440 && e.ai3 < 580) {
416	      const n = e.ai3 - 440;
417	      if (n % 20 === 0) {
418	        const dirx = spriteDir;
419	        const delta = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.7853982;   // ±22.5° 散布（:36133-36136）
420	        shootM(game, e.cx + dirx * 36, e.cy + 8,
421	          Math.cos(delta) * 8 * dirx, Math.sin(delta) * 8 * dirx, 50, 448);
422	      }
423	    }
424	    return;
425	  }
426	  if (id === 393) {
427	    // 激光窗 [280,420)（:36152-36197）：窗内每 6t 一发 449 速 16;窗外只瞄准(ai[2] 角)
428	    const inWin = e.ai3 >= 280 && e.ai3 < 420;
429	    if (!inWin && player && !player.dead) {
430	      let dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
431	      if (dy < 0) dy = 0;
432	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
433	      if (d > 0.001) e.ai2 = Math.atan2(dy / d, dx / d);
434	      else e.ai2 = Math.PI / 2;
435	    }
436	    if (inWin) {
437	      const n = e.ai3 - 280;
438	      if (n % 6 === 0) {
439	        const ang = e.ai2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * (0.7853982 / 3);
440	        shootM(game, e.cx, e.cy, Math.cos(ang) * 16, Math.sin(ang) * 16, 35, 449);
441	        game.playSfxFiles?.(['Item_12'], 1, e.cx, e.cy);     // :36140 部件激光(392 段 Item12)
442	      }
443	    }
444	    return;
445	  }
446	  // ---- 392 炮体（:36199-36286）----
447	  // 部件全灭检测（:35791-35820）：炮塔/火炮全灭 → 普通模式飞碟 ai0=3 自毁(expert→1)
448	  {
449	    const list = slots.get(e);
450	    const turrets = list ? list.slice(0, 2).filter((p) => !p.dead && p.vanillaId === 393) : [];
451	    const cannons = list ? list.slice(2, 4).filter((p) => !p.dead && p.vanillaId === 394) : [];
452	    let allDead = saucer.ai0 >= 1 || saucer.ai0 < 0 ? false : true;
453	    if (allDead && (turrets.length > 0 || cannons.length > 0)) allDead = false;
454	    if (allDead && saucer.ai0 === 0) {
455	      if (!EXPERT) { saucer.ai0 = 3; saucer.ai1 = 0; saucer.ai2 = 0; saucer.ai3 = 0; }
456	      else { saucer.ai0 = 1; saucer.ai1 = 0; saucer.ai2 = 0; saucer.ai3 = 0; }
457	    }
458	  }
459	  // 死光电报（:36201-36219）：巡航态 ai[3]∈[20,260) 首帧（n==0）→ Projectile 447
460	  if (e.ai3 >= 20 && e.ai3 < 260 && saucer.ai0 === 0) {
461	    if (e.ai3 - 20 === 0) {
462	      // 447 死光电报实体（AI_079）——束体独立类（锚定电报塔/母舰，束高逐tick重算）
463	      const ray = new MartianDeathray(e, 50);
464	      (game as unknown as { entities: { add(el: unknown, b: string): void } }).entities.add(ray, 'projectiles');
465	    }
466	  }
467	  // 空巢发射器（:36221-36279）：窗口内对已灭槽位按概率补射 450 导弹
468	  {
469	    const list = slots.get(e) ?? [];
470	    const t0 = list[0], t1 = list[1], c0 = list[2], c1 = list[3];
471	    const t0Dead = !t0 || t0.dead || t0.vanillaId !== 393;
472	    const t1Dead = !t1 || t1.dead || t1.vanillaId !== 393;
473	    const c0Dead = !c0 || c0.dead || c0.vanillaId !== 394;
474	    const c1Dead = !c1 || c1.dead || c1.vanillaId !== 394;
475	    let flag76 = false, mv4 = 1000, mv5 = 1000;
476	    if (saucer.ai0 === 2) { flag76 = true; mv4 = 120; mv5 = 120; }
477	    if (!flag76 && e.ai3 >= 280 && e.ai3 < 400) { flag76 = true; mv5 = 90; mv4 = 60; }
478	    if (!flag76 && e.ai3 >= 440 && e.ai3 < 580) { flag76 = true; mv5 = 60; mv4 = 90; }
479	    if (flag76) {
480	      // 炮塔空巢（:36243-36258）:下方侧向抛射
481	      if (t0Dead && rnd(mv4) === 0) {
482	        const ang = Math.atan2(1, -Math.random() * 3) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.7853982;
483	        shootM(game, e.cx - rndRange(50, 70), e.cy + rndRange(30, 45),
484	          Math.cos(ang) * 3, Math.sin(ang) * 3, 30, 450);
485	      }
486	      if (t1Dead && rnd(mv4) === 0) {
487	        const ang = Math.atan2(1, Math.random() * 3) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.7853982;
488	        shootM(game, e.cx + rndRange(50, 70), e.cy + rndRange(30, 45),
489	          Math.cos(ang) * 3, Math.sin(ang) * 3, 30, 450);
490	      }
491	      // 火炮空巢（:36261-36276）:上方侧向抛射
492	      if (c0Dead && rnd(mv5) === 0) {
493	        const ang = Math.atan2(-1, -Math.random() * 2) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.7853982;
494	        shootM(game, e.cx - rndRange(30, 60), e.cy + rndRange(-30, -10),
495	          Math.cos(ang) * 3, Math.sin(ang) * 3, 30, 450);
496	      }
497	      if (c1Dead && rnd(mv5) === 0) {
498	        const ang = Math.atan2(-1, Math.random() * 2) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.7853982;
499	        shootM(game, e.cx + rndRange(30, 60), e.cy + rndRange(-30, -10),
500	          Math.cos(ang) * 3, Math.sin(ang) * 3, 30, 450);
501	      }
502	    }
503	  }
504	}

506	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
507	// AI_074 火星无人机 388 + 日耀 Corite 418（NPC.cs:35452-35744,参数表 :35477-35509）
508	// ai[0] 0=悬浮瞄准(LOS+俯角+距离带 → 锁方向) / 1=蓄力(×0.95,30t) / 2=扑咬(steer 追击) /
509	//        3=自爆(388 专属:64px 贴身或撞块;3t 后炸) / 4=418 扑后恢复
510	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
511	export function martianDroneAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
512	  const corite = e.vanillaId === 418;
513	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
514	  // 参数表（:35477-35509）：388 / 418
515	  const kbMult = corite ? 0.3 : 0.4;
516	  const spd = corite ? 8 : 10;
517	  const hover = corite ? 175 : 200;
518	  const near = corite ? 175 : 0;
519	  const far = corite ? 600 : 750;
520	  const minNear = corite ? 80 : 0;
521	  const accel = corite ? 60 : 30;
522	  const windup = corite ? 20 : 30;
523	  const decay = corite ? 0.75 : 0.95;
524	  const jitter = corite ? 0 : 50;
525	  const dashSpd = corite ? 9 : 14;
526	  const steerN = corite ? 60 : 20;
527	  const steerAcc = corite ? (4 / 15) * 60 : 0;
528	  const minSpd = 7;
529	  const canExplode = !corite;   // flag68（:35491）
530	
531	  // 朝向（:35454-35469）
532	  if (sign(e.vx) !== 0) e.facing = corite ? sign(e.vx) : -sign(e.vx);
533	
534	  if (e.ai0 === 0 && player && !player.dead) {
535	    // ---- 悬浮瞄准（:35540-35610）----
536	    e.def.knockbackResist = kbMult;    // GetKnockbackMultiplier_ScaledByDifficulty
537	    const dx0 = player.cx - e.cx, dy0 = player.cy - e.cy;
538	    const dist = Math.hypot(dx0, dy0);
539	    // 悬停航向（:35546-35552）：直连向量 - 上方 hover - 侧移 near,归一 × spd
540	    const hx = dx0 + (dx0 < 0 ? near : -near), hy = dy0 - hover;
541	    const hd = Math.hypot(hx, hy) || 1;
542	    const vxT = (hx / hd) * spd, vyT = (hy / hd) * spd;
543	    let canHit = losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy);
544	    if (e.ai3 >= 120) canHit = true;                     // :35556-35558
545	    const aimAng = Math.atan2(dy0, dx0);
546	    const angleOk = aimAng > Math.PI / 8 && aimAng < Math.PI - Math.PI / 8;
547	    const tooNear = dist < minNear, tooFar = dist > far;
548	    if (tooNear || tooFar || !canHit || !angleOk) {
549	      // 悬停逼近（:35564-35583）
550	      e.vx = (e.vx * (accel - 1) + vxT) / accel;
551	      e.vy = (e.vy * (accel - 1) + vyT) / accel;
552	      if (!canHit) {
553	        if (!tooNear && !tooFar) e.ai3 += 1;
554	      } else e.ai3 = 0;
555	    } else {
556	      // 锁定扑咬方向（:35585-35591）
557	      e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0;
558	      e.ai2 = (dx0 / (dist || 1)) * spd;
559	      e.ai3 = (dy0 / (dist || 1)) * spd;
560	    }
561	  } else if (e.ai0 === 1) {
562	    // ---- 蓄力（:35592-35617）----
563	    e.def.knockbackResist = 0;
564	    let keep = true;
565	    if (corite) {
566	      keep = Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy) > 2;
567	      if (!keep && player && !player.dead) {
568	        const d = Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) || 1;
569	        e.vx = lerp(e.vx, ((player.cx - e.cx) / d) * 0.1, 0.25);
570	        e.vy = lerp(e.vy, ((player.cy - e.cy) / d) * 0.1, 0.25);
571	      }
572	    }
573	    if (keep) { e.vx *= decay; e.vy *= decay; }
574	    e.ai1 += 1;
575	    if (e.ai1 >= windup) {
576	      e.ai0 = 2; e.ai1 = 0;
577	      let dx = e.ai2 + rndRange(-jitter, jitter + 1) * 0.04;
578	      let dy = e.ai3 + rndRange(-jitter, jitter + 1) * 0.04;
579	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
580	      dx = (dx / (d || 1)) * dashSpd; dy = (dy / (d || 1)) * dashSpd;
581	      e.vx = dx; e.vy = dy;
582	    }
583	  } else if (e.ai0 === 2) {
584	    // ---- 扑咬（:35618-35659）----
585	    e.def.knockbackResist = 0;
586	    e.ai1 += 1;
587	    const farBelow = player && !player.dead
588	      && Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > (corite ? 150 : 100)
589	      && e.cy > player.cy;
590	    if ((e.ai1 >= 30 && farBelow) || Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy) < minSpd) {
591	      e.ai0 = corite ? 4 : 0; e.ai1 = corite ? 45 : 0;
592	      e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
593	      e.vx /= 2; e.vy /= 2;
594	    } else if (player) {
595	      const d = Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) || 1;
596	      const ux = (player.cx - e.cx) / d, uy = (player.cy - e.cy) / d;
597	      const spdNow = Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy);
598	      e.vx = (e.vx * (steerN - 1) + ux * (spdNow + steerAcc)) / steerN;
599	      e.vy = (e.vy * (steerN - 1) + uy * (spdNow + steerAcc)) / steerN;
600	    }
601	    // 撞块自爆（:35650-35656,仅 388）
602	    if (canExplode) {
603	      const st = game.world.store;
604	      const x0 = Math.floor(e.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((e.x + e.w - 0.01) / TILE);
605	      const y0 = Math.floor(e.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((e.y + e.h - 0.01) / TILE);
606	      outer: for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
607	        for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
608	          if (tx >= 0 && ty >= 0 && tx < st.w && ty < st.h && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) {
609	            e.ai0 = 3; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
610	            break outer;
611	          }
612	        }
613	      }
614	    }
615	  } else if (e.ai0 === 4) {
616	    // ---- 418 扑后恢复（:35660-35668）----
617	    e.ai1 -= 3;
618	    if (e.ai1 <= 0) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }
619	    e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;
620	  }
621	  // 贴身自爆（:35670-35678,仅 388:64px 内）
622	  if (canExplode && e.ai0 !== 3 && player && !player.dead
623	    && Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) < 64) {
624	    e.ai0 = 3; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
625	  }
626	  if (e.ai0 === 3) {
627	    // ---- 自爆演出（:35680-35735）：原地膨胀 192×192,3t 后爆炸消散 ----
628	    const cx = e.cx, cy = e.cy;
629	    e.x = cx - 96; e.y = cy - 96;
630	    e.w = 192; e.h = 192;
631	    e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
632	    e.def.damage = 80;                 // GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty(80)
633	    e.spawnAlpha = 255;
634	    e.lightRGB = [0.2, 0.7, 1.1];
635	    game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#6FD7FF', 6, 2.4, { life: 30 });
636	    e.ai1 += 1;
637	    if (e.ai1 >= 3) {
638	      game.playSfx('explosion', 1, cx, cy);   // SoundID.Item14
639	      e.iframes = 0;
640	      e.hp = 0;
641	      e.hurt(9999, 0, 0, game);
642	    }
643	    return;
644	  }
645	  // aiStyle 74 常态无 AddLight（:35452-35580 全块无点光——曾挂 [0.1,0.4,0.6] 造假常光）
646	  fly(e);
647	}

649	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
650	// AI_073 特斯拉炮塔 387（NPC.cs:35340-35451）
651	// ai[1] <120 = 部署（60-120t 渐显,alpha 255→0,部署期无敌）;ai[0] 电击计时:
652	//   <60 递增,受击 -30;==60 → 放电 435(伤 35,速 14,±100px 抖动) 并置 -120 → 180t 循环
653	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
654	export function teslaTurretAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
655	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }   // 原版 ai[0] 自 0 起 → 首射 60t
656	  if (player && !player.dead) e.facing = player.cx >= e.cx ? 1 : -1;
657	  // 地面摩擦（:35343-35349）
658	  e.vx *= 0.93;
659	  if (e.vx > -0.1 && e.vx < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
660	
661	  if (e.ai1 < 120) {
662	    // ---- 部署（:35353-35401）：渐显 + 部署期无敌 + 环绕尘 ----
663	    e.ai1 += 1;
664	    e.spawnAlpha = e.ai1 > 60 ? Math.round((1 - (e.ai1 - 60) / 60) * 255) : 255;
665	    e.iframes = 2;                       // dontTakeDamage = true
666	    if (e.ai1 % 6 === 0) {
667	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#6FD7FF', 1, 0.6, { life: 20 });
668	    }
669	    e.lightRGB = [0.1 * (e.ai1 / 60), 0.5 * (e.ai1 / 60), 0.7 * (e.ai1 / 60)];
670	    e.lightRGBAt = { x: e.cx, y: e.cy - 10 };   // :35404 光源 = Center.Y−10
671	    e.vy = Math.min(e.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
672	    moveAndCollide(e, game.world, e.vx, e.vy);
673	    return;
674	  }
675	  if (e.ai1 === 120) e.ai1 += 1;         // :35403-35406 单帧过门
676	  e.lightRGB = [0.1, 0.5, 0.7];
677	  e.lightRGBAt = { x: e.cx, y: e.cy - 10 };   // :35411 光源 = Center.Y−10
678	
679	  // ---- 电击循环（:35409-35450）----
680	  if (e.ai0 < 60) e.ai0 += 1;
681	  if (e.justHit) e.ai0 = -30;            // 受击打断充电
682	  if (e.ai0 === 60 && player && !player.dead) {
683	    e.ai0 = -120;
684	    const ox = e.cx, oy = e.cy - 10;
685	    let dx = player.cx - ox, dy = player.cy - oy;
686	    dx += rndRange(-100, 101); dy += rndRange(-100, 101);
687	    dx *= rndRange(70, 131) * 0.01; dy *= rndRange(70, 131) * 0.01;
688	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
689	    if (d > 0.001) { dx = (dx / d) * 14; dy = (dy / d) * 14; }
690	    else { dx = 0; dy = -14; }
691	    shootM(game, ox, oy, dx, dy, 35, 435);
692	  }
693	  e.vy = Math.min(e.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
694	  moveAndCollide(e, game.world, e.vx, e.vy);
695	}
696	
697	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
698	// AI_072 泡泡盾 384（NPC.cs:35318-35339）：零速钉在军官 383 中心;军官消亡 → 消散
699	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
700	export function bubbleShieldAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, _player: Player | null) {
701	  void game; void _player;
702	  const owner = e.master;
703	  if (owner && !owner.dead && owner.vanillaId === 383) {
704	    e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
705	    e.x = owner.cx - e.w / 2;
706	    e.y = owner.cy - e.h / 2;
707	    e.lightRGB = [0.1, 0.5, 0.7];
708	  } else {
709	    e.dead = true;   // life=0 + active=false（无掉落）
710	  }
711	}
712	
713	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
714	// AI_049 愤怒云 250（NPC.cs:31742-31810,普查同批顺带）
715	// 悬顶 200px 伺服（accel 0.25,过零双倍）;正下方+LOS → ai[0] 累计,>8 淋一发雨 264
716	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
717	export function angryNimbusAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
718	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; }
719	  if (!player || player.dead) {
720	    e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;
721	    moveAndCollide(e, game.world, e.vx, e.vy);
722	    return;
723	  }
724	  const ACC = 0.25;
725	  let tx = player.cx - e.cx, ty = player.cy - 200 - e.cy;
726	  const d = Math.hypot(tx, ty);
727	  if (d < 20) { tx = e.vx; ty = e.vy; }
728	  else { const s = 4 / d; tx *= s; ty *= s; }
729	  // 分轴伺服（:31758-31789,过零方向翻转时双倍加速）
730	  if (e.vx < tx) { e.vx += ACC; if (e.vx < 0 && tx > 0) e.vx += ACC * 2; }
731	  else if (e.vx > tx) { e.vx -= ACC; if (e.vx > 0 && tx < 0) e.vx -= ACC * 2; }
732	  if (e.vy < ty) { e.vy += ACC; if (e.vy < 0 && ty > 0) e.vy += ACC * 2; }
733	  else if (e.vy > ty) { e.vy -= ACC; if (e.vy > 0 && ty < 0) e.vy -= ACC * 2; }
734	  // 淋雨（:31791-31808）：水平重叠 + 云底在玩家上方 + LOS → 8t 一发
735	  if (e.x + e.w > player.x && e.x < player.x + player.w
736	    && e.y + e.h < player.y
737	    && losClear(game, e.x, e.y, player.x, player.y)) {
738	    e.ai0 += 1;
739	    if (e.ai0 > 8) {
740	      e.ai0 = 0;
741	      const ox = e.x + 10 + Math.random() * (e.w - 20);
742	      shootM(game, ox, e.y + e.h + 4, 0, 5, 20, 264);
743	    }
744	  }
745	  moveAndCollide(e, game.world, e.vx, e.vy);
746	}
747	
748	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
749	// AI_003 火星尾段：军官 383（cs:56614-56645）/ 工程师 386（cs:59384-59428）
750	// 由分发表 case 3 在 fighterAI 之后调用（原版为 AI_003 内 type 尾段）
751	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
752	export function martianFighterExtras(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
753	  void player;
754	  const id = e.vanillaId;
755	  const enemies = game.enemies() as Enemy[];
756	  if (id === 383) {
757	    // ---- 军官：出生带盾,盾在 → 无敌;盾灭 180t 后补盾（:56614-56645）----
758	    const l0 = la(e);
759	    let shield = officerShield.get(e);
760	    const shieldOk = !!shield && !shield.dead && shield.vanillaId === 384;
761	    if (e.ai2 === 0 && l0[0] === 0 && !shieldOk) {
762	      const s = spawnPart(game, e, 384);
763	      if (s) {
764	        shield = s;
765	        officerShield.set(e, shield);
766	        shield.master = e;          // 原版盾 ai[0] = 军官 index（:56621）
767	        e.ai2 = 1;                  // 原版 ai[2] = 盾 index+1（>0 标记）
768	        l0[0] = -1;
769	      }
770	    }
771	    if (shieldOk) {
772	      e.iframes = 2;                // dontTakeDamage = true（:56629）
773	    } else {
774	      e.ai2 = 0;
775	      if (l0[0] === -1) l0[0] = 180;
776	      if (l0[0] > 0) l0[0] -= 1;
777	    }
778	    return;
779	  }
780	  if (id === 386) {
781	    // ---- 工程师：放炮塔（:59384-59428）----
782	    if (e.confusedT > 0) { e.ai2 = -60; return; }
783	    if (e.ai2 < 60) e.ai2 += 1;
784	    const nT = enemies.filter((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 387).length;
785	    const nE = enemies.filter((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 386).length;
786	    if (e.ai2 > 0 && nT >= 4 * nE) e.ai2 = 0;      // 场上 387 ≥ 4×386（:59393-59396）
787	    if (e.justHit) e.ai2 = -30;                    // :59397-59400
788	    if (e.ai2 === 30) {
789	      // ±5t 随机扫位（:59402-59424）：非中心 2t 禁区 + 实心面 + 上方 4t 净空 + 无岩浆
790	      const st = game.world.store;
791	      const cx0 = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE), cy0 = Math.floor(e.y / TILE);
792	      for (let tries = 0; tries < 100; tries++) {
793	        const tx = rndRange(cx0 - 5, cx0 + 5);
794	        const tyTop = rndRange(cy0 - 5, cy0 + 5);
795	        for (let ty = tyTop; ty < cy0 + 5; ty++) {
796	          const inCore = Math.abs(ty - cy0) <= 2 && Math.abs(tx - cx0) <= 2;
797	          const inSpawn = Math.abs(ty - cy0) <= 0 && Math.abs(tx - cx0) <= 0;
798	          if (inCore || inSpawn) continue;
799	          if (tx < 1 || ty < 1 || tx >= st.w - 1 || ty >= st.h - 1) continue;
800	          if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty)) continue;
801	          if (st.liquid[st.idx(tx, ty - 1)] === 2) continue;   // 上面一格岩浆
802	          let clear = true;
803	          for (let k = 1; k <= 4; k++) if (st.isSolid(tx, ty - k)) { clear = false; break; }
804	          if (!clear) continue;
805	          const turret = spawnPart(game, e, 387);
806	          if (turret) {
807	            turret.x = tx * TILE + 8 - turret.w / 2;
808	            turret.y = ty * TILE - turret.h;   // 底边贴实心面顶（:59425-59426）
809	          }
810	          return;
811	        }
812	      }
813	    }
814	    if (e.ai2 === 60) e.ai2 = -120;                 // :59427-59429
815	  }
816	}

818	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
819	// 火星死光电报 447（Projectile.cs AI_079 :29714-29798 1:1）：垂直向下光束，
820	// 锚定母舰 UFO（ai[0]=npcIdx+1），束高 = 母舰 Y+46 到最近实心格（≤150 格），
821	// 每 tick 重算；母舰不在/电报窗关（395 ai[3]%120 窗）即 Kill。尘 228×2/t。
822	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
823	import { Entity } from './Entity';
824	
825	export class MartianDeathray extends Entity {
826	  w = 30; h = 30;
827	  x = 0; y = 0; vx = 0; vy = 0;
828	  dead = false;
829	  private srcId: number;
830	  private dmg: number;
831	  private age = 0;
832	
833	  constructor(src: Enemy, dmg: number) {
834	    super();
835	    this.srcId = src.id;
836	    this.dmg = dmg;
837	    this.x = src.cx - 15; this.y = src.cy + 46;
838	  }
839	
840	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
841	    if (this.dead) return;
842	    const src = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === this.srcId);
843	    // :29718-29722 门：锚不在场（或非 392/395 电报者/窗口关）→ Kill
844	    if (!src || src.dead || (src.vanillaId !== 392 && src.vanillaId !== 395)) {
845	      this.dead = true; return;
846	    }
847	    if (src.vanillaId === 395 && !(src.ai3 % 120 >= 60 && src.ai0 === 2)) { this.dead = true; return; }
848	    const p = game.player;
849	    if (!p || p.dead) { this.dead = true; return; }   // timeLeft 240 兜底简化
850	    if (++this.age > 240) { this.dead = true; return; }
851	    // :29726-29758 束高：y = 锚 cy+46，向下扫 ≤150 格找首个实心格
852	    const st = game.world.store;
853	    const colX = Math.floor(src.cx / 16);
854	    const startY = Math.floor((src.cy + 46) / 16);
855	    const endY = Math.floor(p.y + p.h);   // num652 = 目标盒底（:29733-29740）
856	    let span = 0;
857	    if (startY >= endY && st.isSolid(colX, startY)) {
858	      span = 1;
859	    } else {
860	      for (; span < 150 && startY + span < st.h; span++) {
861	        const row = startY + span;
862	        if (row >= endY && st.isSolid(colX, row)) { span--; break; }
863	      }
864	    }
865	    this.x = src.cx - this.w / 2;
866	    this.y = src.cy + 46;
867	    this.h = (span + 1) * 16;
868	    // 尘 228×2（:29770-29778，束脚）
869	    if (this.age % 2 === 0) {
870	      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.y + this.h - 16, '#B048E0', 1, 2.8,
871	        { life: 18, grav: 0, size: 3 });
872	    }
873	    // 接触伤害（hostile 447 伤害链简化为玩家盒重叠每 6t 一跳）
874	    if (this.age % 6 === 0
875	      && p.x < this.x + this.w && p.x + p.w > this.x
876	      && p.y < this.y + this.h && p.y + p.h > this.y) {
877	      (game as unknown as { damagePlayer(d: number, x: number, y: number, kx?: number): void })
878	        .damagePlayer(this.dmg, this.cx, this.cy, Math.sign(p.cx - this.cx));
879	    }
880	  }
881	
882	  draw(r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer): void {
883	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
884	    if (!ctx) return;
885	    // 光柱近似（Projectile_447.png 30×4 帧条——竖束以原色半透明柱呈现）
886	    ctx.save();
887	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.75;
888	    const grd = ctx.createLinearGradient(this.x, 0, this.x + this.w, 0);
889	    grd.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(196,84,255,0)');
890	    grd.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(230,160,255,0.9)');
891	    grd.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(196,84,255,0)');
892	    ctx.fillStyle = grd;
893	    ctx.fillRect(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
894	    ctx.restore();
895	  }
896	}
897

Important detail: in vanilla case 0, the fly-position "num13 == 0" branch: `float num14 = (float)Math.Ceiling((player.Center + new Vector2(0f, -100f) - center).Length() / 50f)` — the distance is measured to player.Center + (0,-100), i.e. the player position shifted UP by 100, NOT the actual per-member destination! Ours computes `Math.ceil(Math.hypot(destX - m.cx, destY - m.cy) / 50)` per member — deviation, but the impact is that vanilla's num14 is based on the real cultist's distance to a point 100px above player; ours is per-member distance to each member's own destination. The resulting num14 (number of ticks to travel) differs, thus dash speed differs. Actually wait: in vanilla the vector for each member is `vector = (0,-1).RotatedBy(num16) * (300, 200)`, and velocity = vector2 / num14 where vector2 = player.Center + vector - center2. So each member moves toward its own slot over num14 ticks, where num14 is computed from the leader's distance to player+(0,-100). Ours computes per-member distance to its destination. For the true cultist, vanilla num14 = ceil(dist(center, player+(0,-100))/50), ours = ceil(dist(own center, own dest)/50). These differ by up to ~50px → num14 differs by 1. It's a numeric nuance rather than structural. Not worth reporting maybe; borderline. Actually it does change speed uniformly (all members use same num14 in vanilla). Ours uses different num14 per member. Hmm — this changes arrival simultaneity but mildly. I'd deprioritize.

Also note vanilla case 0 does `if (whoAmI >= nPC2.whoAmI) nPC2.position -= nPC2.velocity;` — ours mirrors with `if (m === e || e.id >= m.id)`. OK.

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-19T03:11:39.316Z · glm-x-preview-260804
