<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Key findings for the ritual circle projectile (490, aiStyle 89):

1. `ai[1] == -1` → alpha increases by 12 (fade out), AND crucially at `ai[0] == 300`, the dragon/squid spawn is gated on `this.ai[1] != -1f`. So when the real cultist is hit during the ritual window, projectile ai[1] = -1 → circle fades out and never spawns the dragon/squidhead. **Our CultistRitualCircle has no such interrupt link** — hitting the real cultist during 120-420 doesn't dismiss the circle, so the circle will still spawn NPC 454/521 at age 300. That's a CONFIRMED deviation: in vanilla, hitting the true cultist before age 300 prevents the dragon spawn (that's the whole "interrupt the ritual" mechanic — actually wait, the ritual interruption mechanic: hitting the CLONE (440) makes cultist vulnerable; hitting the REAL one... let me think about actual game behavior: During ritual, cultist spawns clones; if you hit a clone, it dissipates and the real cultist becomes vulnerable (state 6). If you hit the real cultist, the ritual ends (progress attack cycle) and the circle... in game, hitting the real cultist during ritual causes all clones to vanish and the dragon does NOT spawn (the ritual is interrupted). Yes — ai[1] = -1 prevents spawn. So ours spawning the dragon even when interrupted is a real deviation.

Also our circle dies at age >= 320 unconditionally — vanilla: kills at ai[0]==320 (yes) or when faded with ai[1]=-1. Ours has no fade-out path on interrupt. The minimal fix: when real cultist ritual-hit interrupts (our line ~319-328), signal the circle (e.g. find CultistRitualCircle anchored to e and set a flag) → circle stops spawning dragon at 300 and fades out.

Also note: our circle at age==300 spawns 454 or 521 — the condition `hasDragon ? 521 : 454` matches `!AnyNPCs(454) ? 454 : 521`. OK.

Another detail: vanilla circle dust uses scale-based inward velocity; ours approximates. Fine.

Also vanilla circle checks flag44: anchor NPC must be type 439/454/521 active; ours requires vanillaId===439 (also matches 454/521? After the circle spawns 454, the anchor... note: `this.ai[1] = NPC.NewNPC(...)` — the ai[1] becomes the spawned NPC's index! So after spawning, the circle's anchor becomes the dragon head (454/521). So the circle survives while the dragon is alive and dies when dragon dies. Minor.

Note ours: circle dies at age>=320 regardless. Vanilla kills at ai[0]==320 too (`else if (this.ai[0] == 320f) Kill()`). OK same.

2. Now also important: in vanilla NPC state 5, ai[2] = projectile index of circle. And during state 5, facing is computed toward the CIRCLE's center (not the stored static center). The circle doesn't move, so equivalent. Fine.

3. One more: in state 5 t==30, the NewNPC for clones: `(int)center6.X, (int)center6.Y + height / 2` — ours `spawnCultistClone(game, e, px, py, la.la1)` then inside: `c.x = x - c.w/2; c.y = y + parent.h/2 - c.h;` — NewNPC(x, y) positions NPC with position=(x,y) as top-left... wait actually NewNPC's startX/startY is the position top-left? In Terraria, NPC.NewNPC(src, x, y, type) sets npc.position = (x, y)? Actually NewNPC sets position such that (X, Y) is the top-left? Let me recall: NPC.NewNPC spawns with position = new Vector2(X, Y) — the top-left corner? Hmm, actually in Terraria, NPC.NewProjectile uses center; NewNPC uses top-left position. Let me not spend too much on this; the comment in ours says "X,Y 是底部中心坐标且 Y 再 +真身 height/2" — hmm that's what our port does; vanilla passes center6.X (center X of slot) and center6.Y + height/2. If NewNPC treats (X,Y) as position top-left, then the clone's top-left would be at slot center X — slightly off-center. Our port interprets differently but it's minor positioning nuance. Not structural. Actually wait, in vanilla, `center6 = base.Center + spinningpoint.RotatedBy(...)` — the slot center. NewNPC(x=center6.X, y=center6.Y + height/2). For a clone of same height as cultist, y = slot center Y + height/2 → that's the bottom of the clone if top-left were at slot center... whatever. Skip.

4. Now check the "ai0==5, else if ai[1] in [30,90): flag3/flag4 = true" — invisible & invulnerable. And in [120,420): flag4 = true only (NOT flag3) — meaning alpha=0 visible and... hmm what do flag3/flag4 mean? They're set near the end presumably: flag3 → dontTakeDamage? Let me read the tail of AI_084 (65800-65958) to see flag3/flag4 usage.

Our port: `la.la2 = {x: e.cx, y: e.cy}` then entities.add(new CultistRitualCircle(e)) — CultistRitualCircle constructor takes src.cx/cy at construction... wait, at the time ours constructs, is e already moved? 	private void AI_120_HallowBoss_DashTo(Vector2 targetPosition)
	{
		DirectionTo(targetPosition);
		targetPosition += new Vector2(0f, -300f);
		if (Distance(targetPosition) > 200f)
		{
			targetPosition -= DirectionTo(targetPosition) * 100f;
		}

		float num = vector.Length();
		if (num > 18f)
		{
			num = 18f;
		}
		velocity = Vector2.Lerp(vector.SafeNormalize(Vector2.Zero) * num, vector / 6f, lerpValue);
	}

	public bool Boss_CanShootExtraAt(int playerIndex, int rotationIndexToAttack, int rotationSize, float attackScanDistance, bool alwaysSkipMainTarget = true)
	{
		if (playerIndex == target && alwaysSkipMainTarget)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (playerIndex % rotationSize != rotationIndexToAttack)
		{
			return false;
		}
		Player player = Main.player[playerIndex];
		if (!player.active || player.dead || !playerInteraction[playerIndex])
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (Distance(player.Center) > attackScanDistance)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

## CONFIRMED 偏差(按严重度)

**1. 光之女皇：二阶段连段表抄错，攻击 12 整招缺失(普通模式)**
- 我方:`bossAI_lategame.ts:607` `EMPRESS_CYCLE_P2 = [7,2,8,5,2,6,6,4,8]`
- 原版：NPC.cs:46520-46579。非专家档 num37=9,残差映射为 `[7,2,8,5,2,6,4,8,12]`——`num54` 被连续判两次(:46555-46562)是同一残差(5)的重复检查，只占一拍；而我方把它抄成两个 6。且 `num57→12`(:46576-46579)**没有任何专家门**，只有 11(num51)才是专家专属；我方头注“11/12 为 expert 专属拍，跳过”对 12 是误判。攻击 12 = 873 螺旋弹幕(NPC.cs:47150-47210,Item165、每 6t 自 (−55,−30) 发 `(0,−20).RotatedBy(2π(ai1−10)/50)`、持续 60+90−num17)普通模式二阶段轮换里真实存在。

- 修复：表改 `[7,2,8,5,2,6,4,8,12]`,补 case 12 状态体(可按我方 873 直飞收敛口径)。

**2. 拜月教邪教徒：仪式被打断不清仪式圈 → 幻影龙照召**
- 我方:`bossAI_lategame.ts:317-328`(真身 120-420t 被击中分支)与 `:147-208`(CultistRitualCircle)之间无任何中断链路。
- 原版：NPC.cs:65054 `Main.projectile[(int)ai[2]].ai[1] = -1f`;Projectile.cs:31195-31198(`ai[1]==-1` → alpha +12/t 淡出)、:31282(`ai[0]==300 && ai[1] != -1` 才召 454/521)。

- 置信度：高。

**3. 光之女皇：白天暴怒只实装了 9999 伤害，漏 flag2=true 的全部行为**
- 我方:`bossAI_lategame.ts:646-680`(仅 `damage=9999`)。
